// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#3 — generated proofs use the published kit and runtime ports.
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use super::{Options, pretty};

const README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend proofs\n\n`npm run frontend:proof` builds its own dist, then proves the starter in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\nDay-two scaffolding is opt-in: `snapback4 init --adapt --day2 --apply` captures\ncurrent sources and fixtures in versions/v1 and versions/v2 and adds day2:proof.\nReplace its BUSINESS_ASSERTION_BEGIN block before running `npm run day2:proof`.\nSet PORT_LO and PORT_HI at run time to confine proof servers and launchers.\nSet CHROME=/path/to/chromium or install Playwright's Chromium.\n";
const PREVIOUS_30_README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend and day-two proofs\n\nRun\n`npm run frontend:proof` (it builds its own dist) to prove sign-in and the starter form in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\n`npm run day2:proof` proves saved-source deploy,\nrollback and compatible forward using versions/v1 and versions/v2. Replace the marked business assertion before it can pass.\nSet `PORT_LO` and `PORT_HI` at run time to confine proof servers and launchers;\nunset means allocated proof ports and the interactive starter defaults.\nSet `CHROME=/path/to/chromium` or install Playwright's Chromium (`npx playwright-core install chromium`).\n";
const PREVIOUS_README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend and day-two proofs\n\nAfter `npm run build` (web) or `npm run build:web` (Expo), run\n`npm run frontend:proof` to prove sign-in and the starter form in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\n`npm run day2:proof -- versions/v1.q versions/v2.q` proves saved-source deploy,\nrollback and compatible forward without replacing your working schema.\nSet `PORT_LO` and `PORT_HI` at run time to confine proof servers and launchers;\nunset means allocated proof ports and the interactive starter defaults.\nSet `CHROME=/path/to/chromium` or install Playwright's Chromium (`npx playwright-core install chromium`).\n";
/// Only the exact generated appendix is ignored for historical byte recognition.
pub(super) fn without_readme(text: &str) -> &str { text.strip_suffix(README_PROOFS).or_else(|| text.strip_suffix(PREVIOUS_30_README_PROOFS)).or_else(|| text.strip_suffix(PREVIOUS_README_PROOFS)).unwrap_or(text) }
pub(super) fn readme(mut text: String) -> String {
    if !text.contains("`npm run frontend:proof`") { text.push_str(README_PROOFS); }

}

pub(super) fn files(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str, String)>, options: &Options, backend: u16, frontend: u16, web: bool, _shape: &super::starter::Shape) {
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut().filter(|(name, _)| *name == "README.md") {
        *text = readme(std::mem::take(text)); let _ = name;
    }
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut() {
        if *name == "vite.config.mts" { *text = runtime_vite(text, backend, frontend); }
    }
    let proof = frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), options.flags.get("template").map(String::as_str), web);
    let dir = options.positionals.first().map(std::path::Path::new).unwrap_or_else(|| std::path::Path::new("."));
    let sources = if let Ok(Some(own)) = super::own_schema(dir) {
        own.files.iter().filter_map(|name| std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("snapback").join(name)).ok().map(|text| (name.clone(), text))).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    } else { files.iter().find(|(name, _)| *name == "snapback/schema.q").map(|(_, text)| vec![("schema.q".to_owned(), text.clone())]).unwrap_or_default() };
    let day2 = options.flags.contains_key("day2");
    for version in ["v1", "v2"].into_iter().filter(|_| day2) {
        if dir.join("versions").join(version).exists() { continue; }
        // Init is a one-shot CLI operation. Destinations live for this invocation's
        // plan, whose existing interface uses static generated names.
        for (name, text) in &sources {
            let path: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/{name}").into_boxed_str());
            files.push((path, text.clone()));
        }
        let marker: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/fixtures/.gitkeep").into_boxed_str());
        files.push((marker, String::new()));
        let mut fixtures = files.iter().filter_map(|(name, text)| name.strip_prefix("fixtures/").map(|name| (name.to_owned(), text.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if fixtures.is_empty() {
            if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("fixtures")) {
                for entry in entries.flatten().filter(|entry| entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == "jsonl") || entry.file_name() == "test.sweep.json") {
                    if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(entry.path()) { fixtures.push((entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned(), text)); }
                }
            }
        }
        for (name, text) in fixtures {
            let path: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/fixtures/{name}").into_boxed_str());
            files.push((path, text));
        }
    }
    files.push(("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", proof));
    if day2 { files.push(("scripts/day2-proof.mjs", include_str!("day2-proof.mjs").to_owned())); }
    files.push(("scripts/launch.mjs", include_str!("launch.mjs").replace("BACKEND", &backend.to_string()).replace("FRONTEND", &frontend.to_string())));
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut().filter(|(name, _)| *name == "package.json") {
        let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).unwrap();
        package["scripts"]["frontend:proof"] = json!("node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
        if day2 { package["scripts"]["day2:proof"] = json!("node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1 versions/v2"); }
        if (package["snapback4"].is_null() || package["snapback4"].is_object()) && (package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_null() || package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_object()) {
            package["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"] = json!(day2);
        }
        package["scripts"]["proof:web"] = json!("node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
        package["devDependencies"]["playwright-core"] = json!("1.55.1");
        package["scripts"]["backend"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs backend");
        if web {
            package["scripts"]["dev"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs vite");
            package["scripts"]["preview"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs vite-preview");
        } else {
            package["scripts"]["start"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs expo");
            package["scripts"]["web"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs expo-web");
            package["scripts"]["native:smoke"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs native-smoke");
        }
        *text = pretty(&package);
        let _ = name;
    }
}

pub(super) fn frontend_proof(source: &str, template: Option<&str>, web: bool) -> String {
    // Social keeps account controls on its Profile tab; prove the actual route.
    let mut proof = source.replace("// TEMPLATE_READY", if template == Some("social") {
        "await page.getByText(\"Profile\", { exact: true }).click();"
    } else { "" });
    if template == Some("social") {
        proof = proof.replace("getByLabel(\"Task\"", "getByLabel(\"Display name\"")
            .replace("name: \"Add\"", "name: \"Save profile\"")
            .replace("beginForm(\"add\", { title }", "beginForm(\"saveProfile\", { name: title }");
    } else if template == Some("chat") {
        proof = proof.replace("    const task =", r#"    const viewer = await page.evaluate(() => window.__snapback4Witness.viewer());
    const seeded = await fetch(`${dev.url}/seed`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify([
      { table: "conversations", row: { id: "proof-room", title: "Proof room", creatorId: viewer } },
      { table: "memberships", row: { id: "proof-member", conversationId: "proof-room", principal: viewer, role: "admin", joinedAt: Date.now() } }
    ]) });
    assert.ok(seeded.ok, await seeded.text());
    await page.getByText("Proof room", { exact: true }).click();
    const task ="#)
            .replace("page.getByLabel(\"Task\", { exact: true })", "page.getByPlaceholder(\"Message\", { exact: true })")
            .replace("name: \"Add\"", "name: \"Send\"")
            .replace("beginForm(\"add\", { title }", "beginForm(\"send\", { conversationId: \"proof-room\", body: title }");
        // Chat receipts echo drafts. Restrict evidence to its message display.
        if web { proof = proof.replace("renderedText(page, new RegExp(title))", "renderedText(page, new RegExp(title), { within: page.getByRole(\"region\", { name: \"Thread\" }) })"); }
    }
    let scope = match (template, web) {
        (Some("social"), _) => "page.getByTestId(\"profile-results\")",
        (Some("chat"), true) => "page.getByRole(\"region\", { name: \"Thread\" })",
        (Some("chat"), false) => "page.getByTestId(\"thread-results\")",
        (_, true) => "page.getByRole(\"list\")",
        (_, false) => "page.getByTestId(\"todo-results\")",
    };
    proof = proof.replace("RESULTS_SCOPE", scope);
    proof = proof.replace("BUILD_SCRIPT", if web { "build" } else { "build:web" });
    proof
}

fn previous_frontend(dir: &std::path::Path, web: bool) -> Option<Option<&'static str>> {
    super::proof_advice::frontend_template(dir, web).filter(|(version,_)| matches!(*version,"0.4.28"|"0.4.29"|"0.4.30"|"0.4.31")).map(|(_,template)|template)
}

/// Adapt adds missing proof infrastructure, preserving files and script names
/// already owned by the application. It never runs a package manager.
pub(super) fn plan(dir: &std::path::Path, own: &super::OwnSchema, enable_day2: bool) -> std::io::Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
    let web = dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file();
    let router = dir.join("app/_layout.tsx").is_file();
    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() && !router { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let shape = super::starter::Shape { title: super::title(dir), password: own.identity.is_some_and(|(p, g)| p && !g),
        personas: own.identity.is_none().then(|| own.personas.clone()).filter(|p| !p.is_empty()),
        body: super::starter::Body::Home(None), offline: own.offline.clone() };
    let path = dir.join("package.json");
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)?;
    let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
    let router = router && !web && package["main"] == "expo-router/entry";
    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() && !router { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let original = package.clone();
    let day2_text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs")).ok();
    let referenced_day2 = package["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v| v.as_str().is_some_and(|s| s.contains("scripts/day2-proof.mjs"))));
    let missing_managed_day2 = day2_text.is_none() && matches!(package["scripts"]["day2:proof"].as_str(),
        Some("node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1.q versions/v2.q" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1 versions/v2"));
    let managed_day2 = day2_text.as_deref().is_some_and(super::proof_advice::known_day2) || missing_managed_day2;
    let day2 = enable_day2 || referenced_day2 || package.pointer("/snapback4/starter/day2") != Some(&json!(false)) && managed_day2;
    // Deleting both the command and file records a durable opt-out on apply.
    // Preview/check never write the inferred choice. Explicit --day2 opts in again.
    if (package["snapback4"].is_null() || package["snapback4"].is_object()) && (package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_null() || package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_object()) {
        package["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"] = json!(day2);
    }
    let backend = package["scripts"]["backend"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("snapback4 dev --port ")).and_then(|s| s.parse::<u16>().ok())
.or_else(|| launcher_port(dir, "backend")).unwrap_or(4400);
    let frontend = launcher_port(dir, "frontend").or_else(|| package["scripts"][if web { "dev" } else { "start" }].as_str()
        .and_then(|s| s.split_once("--port ")).and_then(|(_, rest)| rest.split_whitespace().next()).and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())
        .or_else(|| std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("vite.config.mts")).ok().and_then(|s| s.split_once("server: { port: ").and_then(|(_, rest)| rest.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).next()).and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())))
        ).unwrap_or(if backend != 4400 { backend.saturating_add(1) } else if web { 5173 } else { 8081 });
    let mut generated = vec![("package.json", pretty(&package))];
    let mut flags = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    if router { flags.insert("template".into(), "social".into()); }
    if day2 { flags.insert("day2".into(), String::new()); }
    files(&mut generated, &Options { positionals: vec![dir.to_string_lossy().into_owned()], flags, json: false }, backend, frontend, web, &shape);
    // A saved version is immutable application evidence. Never fill its missing
    // fixture files from today's working release during an adaptation.
    generated.retain(|(name, _)| !name.starts_with("versions/") || {
        let version = name.split('/').nth(1).unwrap();
        !dir.join("versions").join(version).exists()
    });
    // New directories capture the current source and contemporary fixtures. Flat
    // legacy snapshots remain untouched evidence; they are never propagated.
    // Custom proofs own their saved source layout; do not manufacture snapshots.
    if !managed_day2 && !enable_day2 { generated.retain(|(name,_)| !name.starts_with("versions/")); }
    let wanted: Value = serde_json::from_str(&generated[0].1).unwrap();
    for group in ["scripts", "devDependencies"] {
        if !package[group].is_object() { package[group] = json!({}); }
        for (key, value) in wanted[group].as_object().unwrap() {
            let old = package[group].get(key).and_then(Value::as_str);
            let managed = group == "scripts" && old.is_some_and(|old| match key.as_str() {
                "day2:proof" => managed_day2 && matches!(old, "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1.q versions/v2.q"),
                "backend" => old.strip_prefix("snapback4 dev --port ").is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
                "dev" | "preview" => old.strip_prefix(if key == "dev" { "vite --port " } else { "vite preview --port " }).and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix(" --strictPort")).is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
                "start" | "web" => old.strip_prefix(if key == "start" { "expo start --port " } else { "expo start --web --port " }).is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
                _ => false,
            });
            if managed || ((key == "day2:proof" && enable_day2 || key == "frontend:proof" && super::proof_advice::known_frontend(dir) || key == "playwright-core" && (super::proof_advice::known_frontend(dir) || super::proof_advice::referenced(dir, "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"))) && old.is_none()) { package[group][key] = value.clone(); }
        }
    }
    let mut config_changes = Vec::new();
    if web {
        if let Ok(current) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("vite.config.mts")) {
            // Replace only the exact initializer forms, allowing their port literals.
            let numbers = current.split("port: ").skip(1).filter_map(|s| s.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).next()?.parse::<u16>().ok()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let mut normalized = current.clone();
            for number in &numbers { normalized = normalized.replace(&format!("port: {number}"), "port: 5173"); }
            let reference = include_str!("starter/web/vite.config.mts").replace("port: 4173", "port: 5173");
            if normalized == reference { config_changes.push(("vite.config.mts", runtime_vite(&current, backend, numbers.first().copied().unwrap_or(5173)))); }
        }
    }
    let inherited_frontend = previous_frontend(dir, web);
    if let Some(template) = inherited_frontend {
        if let Some((_, text)) = generated.iter_mut().find(|(name, _)| *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs") {
            *text = frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), template, web);
        }
    }
    let referenced_after = |path: &str| package["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v|v.as_str().is_some_and(|s|s.contains(path))));
    let mut additions: Vec<_> = generated.into_iter().skip(1)
        .filter(|(name, _)| {
            if *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs" { return inherited_frontend.is_some() || !dir.join(name).exists() && referenced_after(name); }
            if *name == "scripts/day2-proof.mjs" { return day2 && (managed_day2 || !dir.join(name).exists() && (enable_day2 || referenced_day2)); }
            if !dir.join(name).exists() {
                return name.starts_with("versions/") || referenced_after(name);
            }
            false
        }).collect();
    additions.extend(config_changes);
    if package != original { additions.push(("package.json", pretty(&package))); }
    Ok(additions)
}

fn launcher_port(dir: &std::path::Path, name: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/launch.mjs")).ok()?;
    // Recognize the generated fallback expression, not client application code.
    let marker = if name == "backend" { "low === null ? " } else { ": low === null ? " };
    text.split_once(marker)?.1.split_whitespace().next()?.parse().ok()
}


pub(super) fn apply(dir: &std::path::Path, additions: &[(&str, String)]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    for (name, body) in additions {
        let path = dir.join(name);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap())?;
        if *name == "package.json" || *name == "vite.config.mts" || *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs" || *name == "scripts/day2-proof.mjs" { super::replace_file(&path, body)?; }
        else {
            use std::io::Write;
            let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).open(&path)?;
            file.write_all(body.as_bytes())?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Environment is evaluated whenever Vite loads the config, never at init time.
pub(super) fn runtime_vite(text: &str, backend: u16, frontend: u16) -> String {
    if text.contains("const proofRange") { return text.to_owned(); }
    let header = format!(r#"const proofRange = [process.env.PORT_LO, process.env.PORT_HI];
if (proofRange.some(v => v !== undefined) && (!proofRange.every(v => v && /^\d+$/.test(v)) || Number(proofRange[0]) < 1 || Number(proofRange[1]) > 65535 || Number(proofRange[0]) >= Number(proofRange[1]))) throw Error("PORT_LO/PORT_HI must provide at least two valid ports");
const backendPort = proofRange[0] === undefined ? {backend} : Number(proofRange[0]);
const frontendPort = proofRange[0] === undefined ? {frontend} : backendPort + 1;
process.env.VITE_SNAPBACK_URL ??= `http://127.0.0.1:${{backendPort}}`;
"#);
    let mut result = text.to_owned();
    // All generated configurations have literal ports; preserve unrelated config.
    for port in [frontend, 5173, 4173] { result = result.replace(&format!("port: {port}"), "port: frontendPort"); }
    format!("{header}{result}")
}

mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn historical_frontend_templates_keep_assertions_and_gain_their_own_build() {
        for (version, source) in [
            (28, include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.28.mjs")),
            (29, include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.29.mjs")),
            (30, include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.30.mjs")),
            (31, include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.31.mjs")),
        ] {
            for web in [false, true] {
                for template in [None, Some("chat"), Some("social")] {
                    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("day3-historical-proof-{}-{version}-{web}-{template:?}", std::process::id()));
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("scripts")).unwrap();
                    let screen = if web { "src/App.tsx" } else { "App.tsx" };
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join(screen).parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
                    std::fs::write(dir.join(screen), "// screen").unwrap();
                    std::fs::write(dir.join("package.json"), r#"{"scripts":{}}"#).unwrap();
                    std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), "query alive():\n  return true\n").unwrap();
                    let path = dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
                    std::fs::write(&path, frontend_proof(source, template, web)).unwrap();
                    let own = super::super::own_schema(&dir).unwrap().unwrap();
                    let changes = plan(&dir, &own, false).unwrap();
                    let updated = &changes.iter().find(|(name, _)| *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs").unwrap().1;
                    assert_eq!(updated, &frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), template, web));
                    assert!(updated.contains(if web { "[\"run\", \"build\"]" } else { "[\"run\", \"build:web\"]" }));
                    if template == Some("social") { assert!(updated.contains("Save profile") && updated.contains("getByText(\"Profile\"")); }
                    if template == Some("chat") { assert!(updated.contains("Proof room") && updated.contains("name: \"Send\"")); }
                    std::fs::write(path, format!("{}\n// app-owned assertion\n", frontend_proof(source, template, web))).unwrap();
                    assert!(!plan(&dir, &own, false).unwrap().iter().any(|(name, _)| *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"));
                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Runs outside a checkout, using only packed release artifacts. Registry access
// is needed for Expo/Vite dependencies; npm prefers cached downloads when present.
export async function proveStarters(client, platform, expo, directory, browserProofs = true) {
  const env = { ...process.env, CI: "1", EXPO_NO_TELEMETRY: "1", EXPO_OFFLINE: "1", npm_config_audit: "false", npm_config_fund: "false" };
  delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;
  const deadline = performance.now() + 1_200_000;
  const run = (cmd, args, cwd, overrides = {}) => {
    const remaining = Math.ceil(deadline - performance.now());
    if (remaining <= 0) throw Error("starter qualification exceeded its twenty-minute guard");
    return runToolchainCommand(cmd, args, { cwd, env: { ...env, ...overrides }, timeout: Math.min(300_000, remaining) });
  };

//! Immutable source releases with a database-authoritative activation pointer.
use super::*;
use crate::store_owner::Owner;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
#[path = "staging.rs"]
mod staging;
struct Stage(PathBuf);
impl Drop for Stage {fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}

fn directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    options
        .positionals
        .first()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .ok_or_else(|| "a deployment directory is required".into())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let requested = directory(options)?;
    if !requested.exists() {
        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(dir)
}

/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
}
fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    if database.exists() {
        let store = owner
            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
            return release
                .parse()
                .map(Some)
                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
        }
    }
    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
        Ok(text) => text
            .trim()
            .parse()
            .map(Some)
            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    let project = Project { root };
    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    }
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Err(bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .map(ToString::to_string)
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join("\n"));
    }
    Ok(bound)
}
fn check_change(
    dir: &Path,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    options: &Options,
    target_release: Option<u64>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
        for line in lines {
            eprintln!("{line}");
        }
    }
    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
        eprintln!(
            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
            dir.display()
        );
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
            &dir.join(".release-command"),
            true,
            0,
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        file.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(file)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))]
    Err("release commands require Unix".into())
}
fn target(dir: &Path) -> Result<Option<Owner>, String> {
    match deployment_lock(dir) {
        Ok(owner) => Ok(Some(owner)),
        Err(error) if error.contains("E_STORE_BUSY") => Ok(None),
        Err(error) => Err(error),
    }
}

    dir: &Path,
    owner: Option<&Owner>,

        serde_json::to_writer(&mut bytes, &reply).expect("the fixed HTTP refusal and sync identity fit the response ceiling");
        200
    } else { status };

    trace::mark("encode_end");
    let _evidence = dev_refusals::response::response(&bytes.0);

    if let Some(shared) = shared {

        // @ref LLP 3000.023#11 — discover compiler dependency summaries without probing older servers.
        response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Settlement", "1").unwrap());
        response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "X-Snapback-Settlement, X-Snapback-Generation").unwrap());
    }
    if let Some(generation)=generation {response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("X-Snapback-Generation",generation.to_string()).unwrap());}
    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Content-Type", "application/json").unwrap());
    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Cache-Control", "no-store").unwrap());
    for header in extra { response.add_header(header); }
    if allow_origin {
        for header in cors(&request) { response.add_header(header); }
    }
    if let Some(header) = latency::header() { response.add_header(header); }
    trace::mark("write_start");
    if status >= 400 && request.method() == &Method::Post {
        let _ = request.respond_and_close_with_completion(response, |result| latency::finished(result.is_err() || refused));
        return;
    }
    // `into_writer` drops the request reader before it returns. tiny_http
    // prebuffers ordinary bodies up to 1 KiB; other readers may drain on
    // drop, so keep its response-before-drain order for those requests.
    let may_drain = request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > 1024) || request.headers().iter().any(|h| {
        h.field.equiv("Expect") || h.field.equiv("Transfer-Encoding")
            || (h.field.equiv("Connection") && h.value.as_str().split(',').any(|v| v.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("upgrade")))
    });
    if may_drain {
        let _ = request.respond_checked_with_completion(response, |result| latency::finished(result.is_err() || refused));
        return;
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — coalesce buffered JSON HTTP responses
    // The body is already materialized. Frame it before writing so the HTTP
    // library's small socket buffer cannot split headers from a short body
    // and leave the latter waiting for a delayed TCP acknowledgement.
    let mut encoded = Vec::with_capacity(capacity);
    let response = request.prepare_response(response);
    if response.raw_print(&mut encoded, request.http_version().clone(), request.headers(), request.method() == &Method::Head, None).is_ok() {
        let mut writer = request.into_writer();
        trace::mark("write_start");
        latency::finished(writer.write_all(&encoded).and_then(|_| writer.flush()).is_err() || refused);
    }
}

/// A 204 has no representation metadata or advertised bytes. Keeping it out
/// of the JSON response helper also prevents an unread `null` from framing the
/// next response on a persistent connection.
pub(crate) fn respond_empty(request: Request, status: u16) {
    let mut response = Response::empty(status);
    if let Some(header)=generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
    for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(latency::header()).filter(|header| !header.field.equiv("Content-Type")) {
        response.add_header(header);
    }
    let _ = request.respond(response);
}

pub(crate) fn refusal_json(refusal: &Refusal) -> Json {
    serde_json::to_value(refusal).unwrap_or(Json::Null)
}


pub(crate) fn query_params(url: &str) -> BTreeMap<String, String> {
    fn decode(text: &str) -> String {
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(text.len());
        let bytes = text.as_bytes();
        let mut at = 0;
        while at < bytes.len() {
            if bytes[at] == b'%' && at + 2 < bytes.len() {
                let hex = |b: u8| (b as char).to_digit(16).map(|n| n as u8);
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (hex(bytes[at+1]), hex(bytes[at+2])) { out.push(a * 16 + b); at += 3; continue; }
            }
            out.push(if bytes[at] == b'+' { b' ' } else { bytes[at] }); at += 1;
        }
        String::from_utf8(out).unwrap_or_else(|_| text.to_owned())
    }
    url.split_once('?')
        .map(|(_, query)| query.split('&').filter_map(|pair| pair.split_once('=').map(|(k, v)| (decode(k), decode(v)))).collect())
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

// Identity and head are a single snapshot from the owner of the evaluated
// operation. Mutation replay's seq is historical; head is never that receipt.
fn response_head(engine: &mut Engine) -> Result<(String, u64), Refusal> {
    let tx = engine.store_mut().read_transaction()?;
    let id: String = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'store_id'", [], |row| row.get(0)).map_err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::from)?;
    Ok((id, tx.next_seq()? - 1))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.013#6 — a replay belongs to its acknowledged store, even if
// the endpoint changes between the client's probe and this transaction.
fn intent_store(engine: &Engine, payload: &Json) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    let Some(expected) = payload.get("store_id").filter(|id| !id.is_null()) else { return Ok(()); };
    let current = engine.store().meta("store_id")?;
    if expected.as_str().is_some_and(|id| Some(id) == current.as_deref()) { return Ok(()); }
    Err(Refusal::new("E_STORE_REPLACED", "store", "the intent belongs to another store; its original outcome is unknown")
        .site("request.store_id").rewrite("reconcile this write with its original store before submitting new intent").guide("sync"))
}

fn operation_refusal(request: Request, shared: &Shared, status: u16, why: Refusal) {
    let metadata = shared.read_identity();
    match metadata {
        Ok((store_id, head, generation)) => respond_timed(request, shared, status, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why), "store_id": store_id, "head": head, "generation": generation })),
        Err(why) => respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why) })),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — a racing id conflict is a terminal input refusal, not a transport failure.
fn mutation_error_status(refusal: &Refusal) -> u16 {
    if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if matches!(refusal.code.as_str(), "E_ID_REUSED" | "E_DELIVERY_BINDING") { 200 } else { 500 }
}

fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#93-http-admission-and-reporting — lookup, decode and execution share the admitted release.
    let operation = request.url().split('?').next().is_some_and(|p| p.starts_with("/q/") || p.starts_with("/m/"));
    let _scope=crate::release_requests::Scope::begin(*lock(&shared.generation));

    request.set_cleanup_guard(latency::FinishOnDrop);
    #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.request_work);
    let method = request.method().clone();
    let url = request.url().to_owned();
    let path = url.split('?').next().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
    let _evidence = dev_refusals::response::begin(shared, &path);
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — closed read allow-list precedes every handler,
    // declared route, dev door and idempotency replay. Refusals carry no head.
    // Read tokens and malformed credentials always enter this guard. Otherwise
    // authenticated full-token handlers retain their input/refusal ordering.
    // Public and dev doors must resolve any supplied credential before dispatch.
    let public_door = path.starts_with("/dev/") || matches!(path.as_str(), "/seed" | "/auth/signup" | "/auth/login" | "/auth/guest")
        || path == token::ENDPOINT || routes::declared(shared, &url).is_some();
    if request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("authorization")
        && (public_door || crate::auth::parse_bearer(h.value.as_str()).is_none_or(|token| token.starts_with("ro.")))) {
        match principal_of(&request, shared) {
            Ok((_, _, snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read)) if !read_permitted(&method, &path) =>
                return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_READ_ONLY", "auth", "this read session permits reads only; use a full session for writes").guide("read-only")})),
            // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — the private-proof door keeps one indistinguishable refusal.
            Err(_) if path == token::ENDPOINT => return token::denied(request),
            Err(why) => return respond_timed(request, shared, if why.retryable {503} else {401}, json!({"denied": refusal_json(&why)})),
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    if path == token::ENDPOINT { return token::handle(request, shared); }
    // This exact declared POST path is the only non-loopback development
    // exception. Core doors, mismatched methods and query variants stay fenced.
    if method == Method::Post && let Some((name, route)) = routes::declared(shared, &url) {
        return routes::ingress(request, shared, &name, route);
    }
    let ephemeral_request = method == Method::Post && path.strip_prefix("/m/").is_some_and(|op| lock(&shared.bound).schema.operations.get(op).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral));
    let reject = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| {
        if ephemeral_request { ephemeral::failed(request,shared,None,refusal); }
        else if path.starts_with("/q/") { operation_refusal(request, shared, status, refusal); }
        else { respond_timed(request,shared,status,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
    };
    if let Err(refusal) = dev_request(&request, shared) {
        if ephemeral_request { return ephemeral::failed(request, shared, None, refusal); }
        respond_with_sync(request, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }), false, None, Some(shared));
        return;
    }
    // /dev/token has already been routed through its mutual local-owner proof.
    if shared.personas && (path.starts_with("/dev/") || path == "/seed") && !shared.dev_doors() {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&dev_doors::closed())}));
    }
    if path.starts_with("/dev/clock") && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release()) }));
    }
    // Discovered control requests bind to the inspected serving instance.
    // A listener replaced between GET status and POST cannot receive the action.
    if method==Method::Post && matches!(path.as_str(), "/dev/clock/advance"|"/dev/reset"|"/dev/latency/reset") {
        match single_header(&request,"x-snapback-dev-instance") {
            Ok(None)=>{},
            Ok(Some(instance)) if shared.dev_identity.as_ref().is_some_and(|i| i.instance==instance)=>{},
            _=>return respond_timed(request,shared,409,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","dev instance changed since discovery; rerun the command to select the live host"))})),
        }
    }
    if (method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/latency") || (method == Method::Post && path == "/dev/latency/reset") {
        return latency::endpoint(request, shared, method == Method::Post);
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#3-proof-fixture-loading — development field types need no principal.
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/catalog" {
        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "fixture catalog requires dev"))})); }
        let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
        let tables: Vec<Json> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| json!({ "name": name, "fields": table.columns.iter().filter(|(column, _)| !table.private_field(column) && !table.engine_field(column)).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>() })).collect();
        drop(bound);
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "tables": tables }));
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/status" {
        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "status inspection requires dev"))})); }
        // No engine lock: doctor must remain usable while an adoption waits on SQLite.
        let generation = *lock(&shared.generation);
        let personas = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
        let status = lock(&shared.adoption);
        let now = shared.now();
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"instance":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.instance), "initialStoreId":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.initial_store_id), "pid":std::process::id(), "wrapperPid":crate::supervisor::pid(), "storeId":status.store_id, "project":shared.project, "temporary":shared.temporary, "generation":generation, "adoption":status.failure, "now":now, "clockOffsetMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "personas":personas, "scheduling":crate::thread_priority::diagnostics(), "fileDescriptors":crate::host_limits::diagnostics()}));
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/clock/status" { return maintenance::clock_status(request, shared); }
    if method == Method::Options {
        respond_empty(request, 204);
        return;
    }
    if method == Method::Post && path == "/seed" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
    }
    if path == "/dev/reset" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&dev_doors::reset_release()) }));
    }
    // Check identity before upload admission or allocating any large body.
    // The route rechecks after reading, so expiry/revocation during a slow
    // upload cannot retain the authority held at its start.
    // Logout's body is only 4 KiB and its sole authentication happens inside
    // the SQLite writer transaction that closes the live session family.
    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/assets" || path.starts_with("/assets/intent/") || path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/following/") || path.starts_with("/m/") || path.starts_with("/a/")
        || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && path.ends_with("/cancel"))
        || matches!(path.as_str(), "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout-all")) {
        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
            // No authenticated viewer: metadata cannot disclose a store head.
            if path.starts_with("/q/") { return respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
        }
    }
    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/auth/revoke" || path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && !path.ends_with("/cancel"))) {
        if let Err(refusal) = service_of(&request, shared) {
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
        }
    }
    if method == Method::Get && matches!(path.as_str(), "/" | "/schema" | "/api.ts" | "/native.wasm" | "/changes") {
        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
        }
    }
    let _upload_permit = if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
        match crate::assets::admit(shared) {
            Some(permit) => Some(permit),
            None => {
                let mut refusal = Refusal::new("E_RATE_LIMIT", "auth", "too many concurrent uploads; retry shortly");
                refusal.retryable = true;
                return respond_timed(request, shared, 429, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
            }
        }
    } else { None };
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.038#3 — a file upload streams at most the schema's largest file bound, refused before reading when declared larger.
    let file_upload = method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" && crate::assets::is_file_upload(&url);
    if method == Method::Post {
        let ceiling = match path.as_str() {
            "/assets" if file_upload => match crate::assets::file_ceiling(shared) {
                Ok(ceiling) => ceiling,
                Err(refusal) => return reject(request, 415, refusal),
            },
            "/assets" => crate::assets::UPLOAD_CEILING,
            "/seed" | "/dev/reset" => SEED_REQUEST_CEILING,
            "/sync" => SYNC_REQUEST_CEILING,
            path if path.starts_with("/auth/") => crate::auth::BODY_CEILING,
            path if path.starts_with("/following/") => 64 * 1024,
            _ => JSON_REQUEST_CEILING,
        };
        let length = request.body_length();
        let too_large = || if file_upload {
            Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", "asset", format!("the file exceeds {ceiling} bytes, the largest file column's bound")).guide("assets")
        } else {
            Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body exceeds its {ceiling}-byte limit; split it into smaller requests"))
        };
        if length.is_some_and(|length| length > ceiling) {
            return reject(request, 413, too_large());
        }
        let mut buffer = [0; 8 * 1024];
        loop {
            let length = buffer.len().min(ceiling - bytes.len() + 1);
            match request.as_reader().read(&mut buffer[..length]) {
                Ok(0) => break,
                Ok(read) if read > ceiling - bytes.len() => return reject(request, 413, too_large()),
                Ok(read) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..read]),
                Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
                Err(error) => return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("cannot read the request body: {error}"))),
            }
        }
        if length.is_some_and(|length| length != bytes.len()) {
            return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "the request body ended before its declared Content-Length"));
        }
    }
    if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
        return crate::assets::upload(request, bytes, shared);
    }
    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id)=path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix("/declare")){return crate::assets::declare(request,id,bytes,shared);}
    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("/finish")) {
        return crate::assets::finish(request, id, bytes, shared);
    }
    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("/cancel")) {
        return crate::assets::cancel(request, id, shared);
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/assets/intents" { return crate::assets::intents(request, shared); }
    if method == Method::Get && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/") {
        return crate::assets::serve(request, id, shared);
    }
    let body = match String::from_utf8(bytes) {
        Ok(body) => body,
        Err(_) => return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "a JSON request body must be valid UTF-8")),
    };
    let request_now = if (method == Method::Get && path == "/changes")
        || (method == Method::Post && (path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/m/")))
    {
        match shared.advance_time_audiences().map_err(|error| error.refusal()) {
            Ok(now) => Some(now),
            Err(refusal) => return reject(request, 503, refusal),
        }
    } else { None };
    if operation && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
    match (method, path.as_str()) {
        (Method::Post, "/dev/clock/advance") => maintenance::advance(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/jobs/") => maintenance::run_job(request, shared, &path["/jobs/".len()..], &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/dev/jobs/") => maintenance::run_job(request, shared, &path["/dev/jobs/".len()..], &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/a/") => actions::call(request, shared, path.trim_start_matches("/a/"), &body),
        (Method::Post, "/actions/claim") => actions::claim(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, "/actions/claim-set") => actions::claim_set(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, "/routes/verify/claim") => routes::claim(request, shared, &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") => routes::settle(request, shared, path, &body),
        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/actions/") => actions::settle(request, shared, path, &body),
        (Method::Post, "/effects/claim") => effects::claim(request, shared, &body),

/bin/bash -lc 'timeout 10s cat snapback4/crates/snapback4/tests/serve_stage/mod.rs' in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage

use super::*;

const SOURCE: &str = "use identity\ntable items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\njob mark():\n  for item in items:\n    update items[item] { n: 9 }\n";

fn close_doors(shared: &Shared, engine: &mut Engine) {
    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), SOURCE.replace("use identity", "use identity password"))]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    let next = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    lock(&shared.readers).replace(engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
    *lock(&shared.bound) = bound;
    *lock(&shared.generation) = next;
}

#[test]
fn stage_slow_dev_bodies_cannot_retain_authority_after_password_adoption() {
    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
        let before = host.shared.now();
        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(host.address).unwrap();
        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5))).unwrap();
        write!(stream, "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nExpect: 100-continue\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n", payload.len()).unwrap();
        let mut interim = [0; 128];
        let n = stream.read(&mut interim).unwrap();
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&interim[..n]).contains("100 Continue"), "passed the pre-body door fence");
        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut lock(&host.shared.engine));
        stream.write_all(payload.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        let mut response = String::new(); stream.read_to_string(&mut response).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
        assert!(host.shared.now() - before < 5000, "refused clock did not advance");
    }
}

#[test]
fn stage_queued_dev_requests_recheck_authority_at_the_writer() {
    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
        let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        let address = host.address;
        let task = thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10), &format!("POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}", payload.len())));
        wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() > 0);
        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut engine);
        drop(engine);
        let response = task.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn stage_persona_cannot_acquire_a_replacement_password_only_reader() {
    for file in [false, true] {
        let dir = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
        let store = if file { snapback4_core::Store::open(&dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap() }
            else { snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap() };
        let source = "use identity\nuse personas alice\nquery secret():\n  return 1\n";
        let host = Host::with_default_doors_store(source, store);
        let (entered, waiting) = mpsc::channel();
        let barrier = Arc::new((Mutex::new(false), Condvar::new()));
        let held = barrier.clone();
        let once = std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false);
        *lock(&host.shared.reader_open) = Some(Arc::new(move || {
            if once.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) { return; }
            entered.send(()).unwrap();
            let (state, wake) = &*held;
            let mut ready = lock(state);
            while !*ready { ready = wake.wait(ready).unwrap(); }
        }));
        let address = host.address;
        let task = thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10),
            "POST /q/secret HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\nContent-Length: 11\r\n\r\n{\"args\":{}}"));
        waiting.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5)).unwrap();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.replace("use identity", "use identity password").replace("return 1", "return 9876"))]);
        {
            let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
            let generation = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
            lock(&host.shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            *lock(&host.shared.bound) = bound;
            *lock(&host.shared.generation) = generation;
        }
        *lock(&barrier.0) = true; barrier.1.notify_all();
        let response = task.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(status(&response), 401, "file={file}: {response}");
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
        assert!(body(&response).get("data").is_none(), "new result never crosses the closed persona door");
    }
}

#[test]
fn stage_actions_and_session_routes_revalidate_before_durable_admission() {
    let source = "use server\nuse identity\nuse personas alice\naction work()\nroute report: post \"/report\" wait 1s\n";
    for password in [false, true] {
        for path in ["/a/work?wait=0", "/report", "/actions/keep/cancel"] {
            let host = Host::with_default_doors(source);
            // Existing work must remain uncancelled when cancellation loses
            // its persona authority while queued behind the writer.
            {
                let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
                engine.request_action("work", "keep", Context::new("dev:alice", BTreeMap::new()).at(host.shared.now())).unwrap();
            }
            let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
            let before: i64 = engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _requests", [], |row|row.get(0)).unwrap();
            let address = host.address;
            let task = thread::spawn(move || {
                let payload = r#"{"id":"new","args":{}}"#;
                exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10), &format!("POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}",payload.len()))
            });
            wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() > 0);
            let next = if password { source.replace("use identity", "use identity password") }
                else { source.replace("use personas alice\n", "") };
            let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), next)]);
            let generation = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
            lock(&host.shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
            *lock(&host.shared.bound) = bound; *lock(&host.shared.generation) = generation;
            drop(engine);
            let response = task.join().unwrap();
            assert_eq!(status(&response), 401, "password={password}, {path}: {response}");
            assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
            let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
            let after: i64 = engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _requests", [], |row|row.get(0)).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(after, before, "refused admission creates no durable work");
            assert!(!engine.action_request_at("keep", "dev:alice", host.shared.now()).unwrap().cancelled);
            let claims = engine.claim_actions("service:worker", None, 10, 1000, 60, host.shared.now()).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(claims.len(), 1, "only previously admitted work is claimable");
            assert_eq!(claims[0].request.id, "keep");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn stage_asset_owner_transitions_recheck_at_the_writer() {
    let source = "use identity\nuse personas alice\n";
    for path in ["/assets/intent/abc/cancel", "/assets/intent/abc/declare", "/assets/intent/abc/finish", "/assets/intents"] {
        let host = Host::with_default_doors(source);
        let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        let store = engine.store().meta("store_id").unwrap().unwrap();
        let address = host.address;
        let task = thread::spawn(move || {
            let payload = if path.ends_with("declare") { json!({"digest":"a".repeat(64),"placement":{"op":"place","args":{},"argument":"photo"}}) }
                else if path.ends_with("finish") { json!({"op":"place","args":{},"why":{"code":"REFUSED","family":"rule","message":"fixture"}}) }
                else { json!({}) }.to_string();
            let method = if path == "/assets/intents" { "GET" } else { "POST" };
            exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10), &format!("{method} {path}?store_id={store} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}",payload.len()))
        });
        wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() > 0);
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.replace("use identity", "use identity password"))]);
        let generation = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
        lock(&host.shared.readers).replace(&engine,&bound.refusal_sites);
        *lock(&host.shared.bound)=bound;*lock(&host.shared.generation)=generation;
        drop(engine);
        let response=task.join().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"],"E_AUTH","{path}: {response}");
        let engine=lock(&host.shared.engine);
        let intents:i64=engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _asset_intents",[],|row|row.get(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(intents,0,"refused owner request cannot leave an intent or cancellation tombstone");
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — direct children, file logs and bounded cleanup own proof orchestration.
import { spawn, type ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
import { closeSync, mkdirSync, openSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { setTimeout as sleep } from "node:timers/promises";
import { stripVTControlCharacters } from "node:util";

const children = new Set<ChildProcess>();
process.once("exit", () => { for (const child of children) child.kill("SIGKILL"); });
export function register(child: ChildProcess): void {
  children.add(child);
  child.once("exit", () => children.delete(child));
  child.on("error", () => children.delete(child));
}
export interface ProofOptions {
  cwd?: string;
  env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
  args?: readonly string[];
  timeoutMs?: number;
  stopTimeoutMs?: number;
  logsDir?: string;
  /** Keep stdout in memory only (stderr remains diagnostic evidence). */
  secret?: boolean;
  /** Internal: a frontend keeper owns this private process group. */
  detached?: boolean;
  /** Internal: the frontend observes parent death through stdin EOF. */
  livenessPipe?: boolean;
}
export interface ProofHost {
  url: string;
  pid: number;
  logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string };
  stop(): Promise<void>;
}
export function deadline(ms: number | undefined, fallback: number): number {
  const value = ms ?? fallback;
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 1) throw new RangeError("proof timeout must be positive and finite");
  return value;
}
export async function bounded<T>(work: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  try {
    return await Promise.race([work, new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms} ms`)), ms);
    })]);
  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}
/** Attempts every cleanup, even if a preceding step fails or stalls. */
export async function cleanup(...steps: Array<(() => unknown | Promise<unknown>) | undefined>): Promise<void> {
  const errors: unknown[] = [];
  for (const step of steps) if (step) {
    try { await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(step), 10_000, "proof cleanup"); }
    catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
  }
  if (errors.length) throw new AggregateError(errors, "proof cleanup failed");
}
export async function stopChild(child: ChildProcess | Pick<ProofHost, "url" | "stop">, graceMs = 5000): Promise<void> {
  deadline(graceMs, 5000);
  if (child && "url" in child && typeof child.url === "string" && typeof child.stop === "function") {
    await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => child.stop()), graceMs + 5000, "host stop"); return;
  }
  if (!child || !("kill" in child) || typeof child.kill !== "function" || typeof child.once !== "function"
      || !(child.exitCode === null || typeof child.exitCode === "number")
      || !(child.signalCode === null || typeof child.signalCode === "string")) {
    throw new TypeError("stopChild needs a ChildProcess or { url, stop }");
  }
  if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) return;
  let done!: () => void;
  const exited = new Promise<void>(resolve => { done = resolve; });
  child.once("exit", done);
  child.once("error", done);
  try {
    child.kill("SIGTERM");
    try { await bounded(exited, graceMs, "SIGTERM"); }
    catch {
      child.kill("SIGKILL");
      await bounded(exited, 5000, "SIGKILL reap");
    }
  } finally { child.removeListener("exit", done); child.removeListener("error", done); }
}
export function portRange(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): string {
  const low = env.PORT_LO, high = env.PORT_HI;
  if (low === undefined && high === undefined) return "0";
  if (!low || !high || !/^\d+$/.test(low) || !/^\d+$/.test(high)
      || Number(low) < 1 || Number(high) > 65535 || Number(low) > Number(high)) {
    throw Error("PORT_LO and PORT_HI must both specify an inclusive range, 1 <= low <= high <= 65535");
  }
  return `${Number(low)}-${Number(high)}`;
}
/** App-local evidence survives harness cleanup of its temporary root. */
export function logsDir(options: Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> = {}): string {
  return resolve(options.cwd ?? ".", options.logsDir ?? "logs");
}
export function logged(command: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}) {
  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? "."), id = randomUUID();
  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const logs = { stdout: join(directory, `${id}.stdout.log`), stderr: join(directory, `${id}.stderr.log`) };
  if (options.secret) writeFileSync(logs.stdout, "stdout withheld: secret command (returned only to caller)\n", { flag: "wx", mode: 0o600 });
  const out = options.secret ? undefined : openSync(logs.stdout, "wx", 0o600), err = openSync(logs.stderr, "wx", 0o600);
  let child: ChildProcess;
  try { child = spawn(command, [...args], { cwd, detached: options.detached ?? false, env: { ...(options.env ?? process.env), SNAPBACK4_PROOF_LAUNCH: id }, stdio: [options.livenessPipe ? "pipe" : "ignore", options.secret ? "pipe" : out!, err] }); }
  finally { if (out !== undefined) closeSync(out); closeSync(err); }
  // Secret stdout never reaches a file, even briefly. Callers still receive it.
  let stdout = "";
  child.stdout?.setEncoding("utf8");
  child.stdout?.on("data", chunk => { stdout += chunk; });
  register(child);
  child.stdin?.on("error", () => {});
  let error: Error | undefined;
  child.on("error", value => { error = value; });
  const closed = new Promise<void>(resolve => child.once("close", () => resolve()));
  return { child, logs, id, closed, stdout: () => options.secret ? stdout : logText(logs.stdout), error: () => error };
}
export const logText = (path: string): string => readFileSync(path, "utf8");
export async function ready(run: ReturnType<typeof logged>, parse: (line: string) => string | undefined, timeoutMs: number): Promise<string> {
  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let outAt = 0, errAt = 0;
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
    if (run.child.exitCode !== null || run.child.signalCode !== null) {
      throw Error(`proof child exited before ready (${run.child.exitCode ?? run.child.signalCode}); ${run.logs.stderr}\n${logText(run.logs.stderr).slice(-4000)}`);
    }
    for (const [path, offset, stream] of [[run.logs.stdout, outAt, "out"], [run.logs.stderr, errAt, "err"]] as const) {
      const text = logText(path);
      const complete = text.lastIndexOf("\n") + 1;
      for (const line of text.slice(offset, complete).split("\n")) {
        const url = parse(stripVTControlCharacters(line).trim());
        if (url) return url;
      }
      if (stream === "out") outAt = complete; else errAt = complete;
    }
    await sleep(25);
  }
  throw Error(`proof child ready timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; logs: ${run.logs.stdout}, ${run.logs.stderr}`);
}

pub fn serve(

export const productionAccounts = String.raw`
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import {signup} from "snapback4/auth";
import {createClient} from "snapback4/client";
import {startDev, bounded, cleanup} from "snapback4/proof";
import {api} from "./snapback/generated/api.ts";
const clients=[];
let dev;
const request=(input,init={})=>fetch(input,{...init,signal:AbortSignal.any([
  ...(init.signal?[init.signal]:[]),AbortSignal.timeout(10000)])});
const query=(client,op,args)=>bounded(client.query(op,args),30000,op.name);
const mutate=(client,op,args)=>bounded(client.mutate(op,args),30000,op.name);
try {
  dev=await startDev({args:["--no-watch"],timeoutMs:30000});
  async function account(name){
    const signed=await bounded(signup(dev.url,name+"@production.example","production-proof-password",request),15000,"signup "+name);
    assert.equal(signed.ok,true,"password signup succeeds");
    const client=createClient({url:dev.url,session:signed.session,fetch:request});clients.push(client);
    assert.ok(!signed.session.principal.startsWith("dev:"),"signup creates a real account principal");
    const teams=await query(client,api.myTeams,{});
    assert.deepEqual(teams.data,[],"signup grants no team membership");
    assert.equal(teams.existence,"absent");assert.equal(teams.complete,true);
    const missingRole=await query(client,api.myRole,{teamId:"not-enrolled-team"});
    assert.equal(missingRole.denied?.code,"NOT_MEMBER","signup grants no roster role");
    return client;
  }
  const alice=await account("alice"),bob=await account("bob"),outsider=await account("outsider");
  const created=await mutate(alice,api.createTeam,{title:"Production account team"});
  assert.equal(created.state,"sent");assert.ok(created.result?.id,"creation returns a fresh team result");
  const teamId=created.result.id;
  const ownRole=await query(alice,api.myRole,{teamId});
  assert.equal(ownRole.data?.role,"admin","creating a team explicitly grants its creator admin");
  assert.equal(ownRole.data?.principal,alice.viewer());
  for(const client of [bob,outsider]){
    assert.equal((await query(client,api.myRole,{teamId})).denied?.code,"NOT_MEMBER");
    assert.equal((await query(client,api.roster,{teamId})).denied?.code,"NOT_MEMBER","uninvited accounts cannot enumerate the roster");
    const forged=await mutate(client,api.invite,{teamId,principal:client.viewer(),role:"admin"});
    assert.equal(forged.state,"failed");assert.equal(forged.why.code,"NOT_ADMIN","signup cannot self-grant a role");
  }
  const invited=await mutate(alice,api.invite,{teamId,principal:bob.viewer(),role:"member"});
  assert.equal(invited.state,"sent");
  const bobRole=await query(bob,api.myRole,{teamId});
  assert.equal(bobRole.data?.role,"member","the authorized invitation grants only its selected role");
  const roster=await query(bob,api.roster,{teamId});
  assert.deepEqual(roster.data.map(row=>[row.principal,row.role]).sort(),[[alice.viewer(),"admin"],[bob.viewer(),"member"]].sort());
  const escalation=await mutate(bob,api.invite,{teamId,principal:outsider.viewer(),role:"admin"});
  assert.equal(escalation.state,"failed");assert.equal(escalation.why.code,"NOT_ADMIN");
  assert.equal((await query(outsider,api.roster,{teamId})).denied?.code,"NOT_MEMBER");
  assert.deepEqual((await query(outsider,api.myTeams,{})).data,[]);
  console.log("packed production accounts passed: signup grants no role, creator admin, principal invitation member, outsider denied");
} finally {await cleanup(...clients.map(client=>()=>client.close()),()=>dev?.stop());}
`;

exec
/bin/bash -lc "timeout 10s sed -n '1,145p' snapback4/scripts/publication-production.mjs" in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage
 succeeded in 0ms:
// Run the production guide's complete starter slice using the installed packs.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdirSync, readdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { basename, dirname, extname, join, resolve, sep } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { runToolchainCommand } from "./publication-starters.mjs";

const ignored = new Set(["node_modules", ".git", "target", "dist", "build", ".expo", ".snapback4", "logs", "fixtures", "versions", "recipes"]);
const frontend = new Set([".ts", ".tsx", ".js", ".jsx", ".mjs", ".cjs", ".html"]);
const sha = bytes => createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex");

/** Only authored schema/frontend sources enter the private external-lane copy.
 * Symlinks are not followed; dependencies, fixtures, logs and outputs stay out. */
export function productionSources(root) {
  const files = new Map();
  function walk(relative = "", depth = 20) {
    assert.ok(depth > 0, `source directory nesting exceeds qualification bound: ${relative}`);
    for (const entry of readdirSync(join(root, relative), { withFileTypes: true }).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
      if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) continue;
      const path = join(relative, entry.name);
      if (entry.isDirectory()) { if (!ignored.has(entry.name) && entry.name !== "generated") walk(path, depth - 1); }
      else if (entry.isFile() && (frontend.has(extname(path)) || (path.startsWith(`snapback${sep}`) && extname(path) === ".q")))
        files.set(path, readFileSync(join(root, path)));
    }
  }
  walk();
  assert.ok(files.has(join("snapback", "schema.q")), `${root} has snapback/schema.q`);
  return files;
}

function snapshot(root) {
  return [...productionSources(root)].map(([path, bytes]) => [path, sha(bytes)]);
}

function privateSnapshot(root) {
  const files = [];
  function walk(relative = "") {
    for (const entry of readdirSync(join(root, relative), { withFileTypes: true }).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
      if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) continue;
      const path = join(relative, entry.name);
      if (entry.isDirectory()) { if (!ignored.has(entry.name) && entry.name !== "generated") walk(path); }
      else if (entry.isFile() && !/^production-.*\.log$/.test(entry.name)) files.push([path, sha(readFileSync(join(root, path)))]);
    }
  }
  walk(); return files;
}

function capture(cli, args, cwd, env, log) {
  const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [cli, ...args], { cwd, env, encoding: "utf8", timeout: 60_000, maxBuffer: 16 * 1024 * 1024 });
  writeFileSync(log, result.stdout + result.stderr);
  assert.equal(result.status, 0, `${args.join(" ")}: ${result.error?.message ?? ""}\n${result.stdout}${result.stderr}`);
  return result.stdout;
}

/** The CLI only receives the private copy's cwd; original lanes are read-only. */
export function proveProductionAdaptation(cli, cwd, env, { original } = {}) {
  const originalBefore = original ? snapshot(original) : undefined;
  const before = privateSnapshot(cwd), frontendBefore = before.filter(([path]) => !path.startsWith(`snapback${sep}`));
  const sourcesBefore = productionSources(cwd);
  const identityBefore = [...sourcesBefore].filter(([path]) => path.startsWith(`snapback${sep}`)).map(([, bytes]) => bytes.toString().match(/^use identity[^\n]*/gm) ?? []).flat();
  const preview = capture(cli, ["init", "--adapt", "--production"], cwd, env, join(cwd, "production-preview.log"));
  assert.match(preview, /preview; no files written/);
  assert.match(preview, /N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER tables/);
  if ([...sourcesBefore].some(([path, bytes]) => !path.startsWith(`snapback${sep}`) && bytes.includes("dev:")))
    assert.match(preview, /hard-coded dev: principal/, "authored development principals are listed for review");
  assert.deepEqual(privateSnapshot(cwd), before, "preview leaves every private application byte untouched");
  const writers = [...preview.matchAll(/: add (\w+); allow service:ops/g)].map(match => match[1]);
  assert.ok(writers.length > 0, "fixture-only rosters receive provisioning mutations");
  const applied = capture(cli, ["init", "--adapt", "--production", "--apply"], cwd, env, join(cwd, "production-apply.log"));
  assert.match(applied, /Signup grants no role/);
  const after = privateSnapshot(cwd);
  assert.deepEqual(after.filter(([path]) => !path.startsWith(`snapback${sep}`)), frontendBefore, "apply preserves every screen and frontend source byte");
  const schema = [...productionSources(cwd)].filter(([path]) => path.startsWith(`snapback${sep}`)).map(([, bytes]) => bytes.toString()).join("\n");
  const identityAfter = schema.match(/^use identity[^\n]*/gm) ?? [];
  assert.deepEqual(identityAfter, identityBefore.length ? identityBefore : ["use identity"], "existing identity policy is preserved; persona-only sources gain bare identity");
  for (const name of writers) {
    assert.equal([...schema.matchAll(new RegExp(`^mutation ${name}\\(`, "gm"))].length, 1, `one ${name} provisioning mutation`);
  }
  assert.match(schema, /require viewer = 'service:ops' else NOT_OPERATOR/);
  capture(cli, ["check"], cwd, env, join(cwd, "production-check.log"));
  capture(cli, ["init", "--adapt", "--production", "--apply"], cwd, env, join(cwd, "production-second-apply.log"));
  assert.deepEqual(privateSnapshot(cwd), after, "a repeated apply is idempotent");
  if (original) assert.deepEqual(snapshot(original), originalBefore, "original evidence lane remains byte-identical");
  return { status: "passed", preview: "unchanged", apply: "compiled", frontend: "unchanged", identity: identityAfter, writers,
    idempotent: true, ...(original ? { original, originalSources: originalBefore, originalUnchanged: true } : {}) };
}

export async function proveProduction(client, platform, expo, directory) {
  const artifacts = [client, platform, expo].map(path => resolve(path));
  const output = resolve(directory), root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), "..");
  mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });
  const env = { ...process.env, CI: "1", EXPO_NO_TELEMETRY: "1", EXPO_OFFLINE: "1", npm_config_audit: "false", npm_config_fund: "false" };
  delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN; delete env.PORT_LO; delete env.PORT_HI;
  const run = (command, args, cwd, overrides = {}) => runToolchainCommand(command, args, { cwd, env: { ...env, ...overrides }, timeout: 300_000 });
  const starters = [];
  for (const kind of ["web", "expo"]) {
    const cwd = join(output, `production-${kind}`); mkdirSync(cwd, { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json"), '{"private":true,"type":"module"}');
    await run("npm", ["install", "--no-save", "--ignore-scripts", "--no-audit", "--no-fund", ...artifacts.slice(0, 2)], cwd);
    const cli = join(cwd, "node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js");
    await run(process.execPath, [cli, "init", `--${kind}`], cwd);
    const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json")));
    pkg.dependencies.snapback4 = `file:${artifacts[0]}`;
    pkg.optionalDependencies = { [`snapback4-${process.platform}-${process.arch}`]: `file:${artifacts[1]}` };
    if (kind === "expo") pkg.dependencies["snapback4-expo"] = `file:${artifacts[2]}`;
    writeFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json"), JSON.stringify(pkg, null, 2));
    await run("npm", ["install", "--prefer-offline", "--no-audit", "--no-fund"], cwd);
    const guide = capture(cli, ["guide", "production", "--full"], cwd, env, join(cwd, "production-guide.log"));
    const blocks = [...guide.matchAll(/```sh\n([\s\S]*?)\n```/g)].map(match => match[1]).filter(text => text.startsWith("cp recipes/production/schema.q"));
    assert.equal(blocks.length, 2, "packed guide carries React and Expo command recipes");
    const commands = blocks[kind === "web" ? 0 : 1].trim().split("\n");
    assert.equal(commands.length, 7, "complete schema/screen/client/check/test/typecheck/build recipe");
    // Execute the actual packed guide lines without changing their arguments.
    // No shell interpolation is needed for these fixed, whitespace-only forms.
    for (const line of commands) {
      const [command, ...args] = line.trim().split(/\s+/);
      assert.ok(["cp", "npx", "npm"].includes(command), `unsupported guide command: ${line}`);
      await run(command, args, cwd);
    }
    starters.push({ kind, cwd, status: "passed", commands, schema: "compiled", savedTests: "passed", typecheck: "passed", build: "passed" });
  }
  const [web, native] = starters;
  writeFileSync(join(web.cwd, "production-accounts.mjs"), productionAccounts);
  await run(process.execPath, ["production-accounts.mjs"], web.cwd);
  await run(process.execPath, ["--test", join(root, "packages/snapback4/test/production-starters.test.mjs")], root, {
    SNAPBACK4_PRODUCTION_WEB_APP: join(web.cwd, "recipes/production/App.tsx"),
    SNAPBACK4_PRODUCTION_EXPO_APP: join(native.cwd, "recipes/production/App.tsx"),
    SNAPBACK4_PRODUCTION_NATIVE_SMOKE: join(native.cwd, "src/native-smoke.ts"),
    SNAPBACK4_PRODUCTION_CLIENT_DIST: join(web.cwd, "node_modules/snapback4/dist"),
  });
  const cli = join(web.cwd, "node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js");
  const persona = join(output, "production-adapt-persona"); mkdirSync(persona, { recursive: true });
  await run(process.execPath, [cli, "init", "--web"], persona);
  const path = join(persona, "snapback/schema.q");
  writeFileSync(path, readFileSync(path, "utf8").replace(/^use identity[^\n]*\n/gm, "") + personaRoster);
  writeFileSync(join(persona, "src/legacy-principal.ts"), "export const legacyPrincipal = 'dev:alice';\n");
  const adaptation = [{ kind: "persona-only starter", ...proveProductionAdaptation(cli, persona, env) }];
  for (const [index, original] of (process.env.SNAPBACK4_PRODUCTION_LANES ?? "").split(":").filter(Boolean).map(path => resolve(path)).entries()) {
    const cwd = join(output, `production-adapt-lane-${index}-${basename(original)}`); mkdirSync(cwd, { recursive: true });
    const before = snapshot(original);
    for (const [path, bytes] of productionSources(original)) { mkdirSync(dirname(join(cwd, path)), { recursive: true }); writeFileSync(join(cwd, path), bytes); }
    assert.deepEqual(snapshot(original), before, "copying does not modify original sources");
    adaptation.push({ kind: "evidence copy", ...proveProductionAdaptation(cli, cwd, env, { original }) });
