// Builds its own dist before starting proof servers.
// Replace the application assertions when replacing the starter screen.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { command, startDev, startStatic, withBrowser, renderedText, fillRN, statusText, waitForFresh, bounded, cleanup, portRange } from "snapback4/proof";

// For SVG/foreignObject controls, click the actual rendered box, not a text
// descendant whose DOM geometry Playwright may interpret differently.
async function clickSvg(page, control) {
  await control.waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  const box = await control.boundingBox();
  assert.ok(box && box.width > 0 && box.height > 0, "SVG control has no rendered box");
  await page.mouse.click(box.x + box.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2);
}

    }
  });
  console.log("frontend:proof passed");

//! Advice is evidence about known template bytes, or explicitly a heuristic.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — custom proofs own their arguments and assertions.
use std::path::Path;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use serde::Deserialize;

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct Template { file: String, version: String, template: Option<String>, web: bool, sha256: String }
fn templates() -> &'static [Template] {
    static TEMPLATES: std::sync::OnceLock<Vec<Template>> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
    TEMPLATES.get_or_init(|| serde_json::from_str(include_str!("proof-digests.json")).expect("shipped proof digests"))
}
fn digest(text: &str) -> String { format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(text.as_bytes())) }
pub(super) fn known_day2(text: &str) -> bool {
    let hash = digest(text);
    templates().iter().any(|t| t.file == "day2" && t.sha256 == hash)
}
pub(super) fn frontend_template(dir: &Path, web: bool) -> Option<(&'static str, Option<&'static str>)> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")).ok()?;
    let hash = digest(&text);
    templates().iter().find(|t| t.file == "frontend" && t.web == web && t.sha256 == hash)
        .map(|t| (t.version.as_str(), t.template.as_deref()))
}
pub(super) fn known_frontend(dir: &Path) -> bool { [false,true].into_iter().any(|web|frontend_template(dir,web).is_some()) }
fn package(dir: &Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("package.json")).ok().and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str(&s).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
}
pub(super) fn referenced(dir: &Path, path: &str) -> bool {
    package(dir)["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v|v.as_str().is_some_and(|s|s.contains(path))))
}
pub(super) fn report(dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut advice = Vec::new();
    for path in ["scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", "scripts/day2-proof.mjs", "scripts/launch.mjs"] {
        if !dir.join(path).exists() && referenced(dir, path) {
            advice.push(format!("info: {path} is missing but package.json still references it; preview `snapback4 init --adapt`, then `snapback4 init --adapt --apply`"));
        }
    }
    if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs")) {
        let hash=digest(&text);
        if let Some(t)=templates().iter().find(|t| t.file=="day2" && t.sha256==hash) {
            if matches!(t.version.as_str(),"0.4.28"|"0.4.29") {
                advice.push(format!("info: scripts/day2-proof.mjs is the unmodified {} starter: it asserts no business rows; preview `snapback4 init --adapt` to replace it and capture current sources/fixtures",t.version));
            }
            if t.version=="0.4.30" || t.version=="0.4.31" {
                let script=package(dir)["scripts"]["day2:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
                if script=="node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" {
                    advice.push("info: the unmodified day-two starter needs its two saved-source arguments; preview `snapback4 init --adapt`".into());
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Ok(text)=std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")) {
        // Custom JavaScript can hide builds in arbitrary imports/functions. These
        // checks deliberately make no claim about its behavior or ownership.
        let active=text.lines().filter(|line| !line.trim_start().starts_with("//")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        let flat=active.replace(['\"','\'',',','[',']','(',')'], " ");
        let words=flat.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let serves_dist=words.windows(2).any(|w|w==["startStatic","dist"]);
        let pkg=package(dir);
        let launch=pkg["scripts"]["frontend:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
        let pre=pkg["scripts"]["prefrontend:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
        let builds=|s:&str|s.contains("build")||s.contains("export");
        if serves_dist && !builds(&active) && !builds(launch) && !builds(pre) {
            advice.push("info: scripts/frontend-proof.mjs serves dist with no visible build step (heuristic; imported code may build it); build in the proof or its npm script before startStatic(\"dist\")".into());
        }
        if words.windows(3).any(|w|w==["npx","snapback4","dev"]) {
            advice.push("info: scripts/frontend-proof.mjs launches npx snapback4 dev (heuristic); use startDev from snapback4/proof for an owned server and bounded cleanup".into());
        }
    }
    advice
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn every_shipped_proof_template_has_a_digest() {
        let mut expected=Vec::new();
        for (version,frontend,day2) in [
            ("0.4.28",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.28.mjs"),include_str!("previous/day2-proof-0.4.28.mjs")),
            ("0.4.29",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.29.mjs"),include_str!("previous/day2-proof-0.4.29.mjs")),
            ("0.4.30",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.30.mjs"),include_str!("previous/day2-proof-0.4.30.mjs")),
            ("0.4.31",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.31.mjs"),include_str!("day2-proof.mjs")),
            ("0.4.32",include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"),include_str!("day2-proof.mjs")),
        ] {
            for web in [false,true] { for template in [None,Some("chat"),Some("social")] {
                expected.push(serde_json::json!({"file":"frontend","version":version,"web":web,"template":template,"sha256":digest(&super::super::proof::frontend_proof(frontend,template,web))}));
            }}
            expected.push(serde_json::json!({"file":"day2","version":version,"web":false,"template":null,"sha256":digest(day2)}));
        }
        if std::env::var_os("SNAPBACK4_UPDATE_PROOF_DIGESTS").is_some() {
            std::fs::write(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),"/src/verbs/init/proof-digests.json"),serde_json::to_string_pretty(&expected).unwrap()+"\n").unwrap();
        } else {
            assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(include_str!("proof-digests.json")).unwrap(),serde_json::json!(expected),"retain historical digests and register each new shipped template");
        }
    }
}

//! Development doors beside the operation routes: `POST /seed`, `POST /dev/reset`, and the
//! `--no-watch` hint that names a schema edited on disk after adoption.
use super::{Shared, lock, refusal_json, respond_timed};
use crate::project::{Project, SourceStamp};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
use snapback4_core::wire;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Mutex;
use tiny_http::Request;

/// The source stamp each `--no-watch` dev host adopted, by project root.
static PINNED: Mutex<BTreeMap<PathBuf, SourceStamp>> = Mutex::new(BTreeMap::new());

pub(super) fn closed() -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "development doors are closed; use serve staging with real users (guide staging), or explicitly opt into dev --unsafe-dev-doors for local fixtures").guide("staging")
}

pub(super) fn pin(project: &Path, stamp: SourceStamp) {
    PINNED.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).insert(project.to_owned(), stamp);
}

/// A `--no-watch` host serves the schema it started with. When a request
/// names an operation or argument that host does not know and the `.q` files
/// changed on disk since, say so: the edit is real, it was just never adopted.
pub(super) fn stale(shared: &Shared, refusal: Refusal) -> Refusal {
    let Some(adopted) = PINNED.lock().unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner()).get(&shared.project).copied() else { return refusal };
    if (Project { root: shared.project.clone() }).sources_stamp() == adopted { return refusal; }
    let message = format!("{}; this dev host runs with --no-watch and snapback/ changed on disk after it adopted its schema, so the edit is not served", refusal.message);
    Refusal { message, rewrite: Some("restart `snapback4 dev` (or drop --no-watch so saves re-adopt), then retry".into()), ..refusal }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#8 — persona names are not their principals; dev only.
pub(super) fn persona_argument(shared: &Shared, kinds: &BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::ir::ArgType>, args: &Json) -> Option<Refusal> {
    if !shared.personas { return None; }
    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
    crate::persona_principals::arguments(&bound.personas,kinds,args)
}
// Rows are written as the system principal; each names its id because seed is an upsert by id.
pub(super) fn seed(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.dev_doors() || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {
        respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
        return;
    }
    let refuse = |request, refusal: Refusal| respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal.guide("personas")) }));
    let rows: Vec<Json> = match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(Json::Array(rows)) => rows,
        _ => return refuse(request, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "seed takes a JSON array of {table, row}")),
    };
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    if let Err(why) = shared.dev_authority(&engine) { drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})); }
    let now = shared.now();
    let typed = match typed_rows(engine.schema(), &rows, now) {
        Ok(typed) => typed,
        Err(refusal) => { drop(engine); return refuse(request, refusal); }
    };
    let outcome = engine.seed_at(typed, now);
    drop(engine);
    match outcome {
        Ok(count) => {
            shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
            respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "seeded": count }));
        }
        Err(error) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&seed_refusal(error)) })),
    }
}

/// `/seed` rows checked against the served schema and typed, or the first problem.
fn typed_rows(schema: &snapback4_core::Schema, rows: &[Json], now: i64) -> Result<Vec<(String, snapback4_core::Value)>, Refusal> {
    let mut typed = Vec::new();
    for (index, entry) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
        let (Some(table), Some(row)) = (entry.get("table").and_then(Json::as_str), entry.get("row")) else {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "each seed entry is {table, row}"));
        };
        let Some(definition) = schema.table(table) else {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("unknown table {table}")));
        };
        if row.is_object() && row.get("id").is_none_or(|id| !id.is_string()) {
            return Err(missing_id(index, table));
        }
        let mut row = row.clone();
        crate::verbs::test_fixture::relative_times(definition, &mut row, now)
            .map_err(|why| Refusal::new("E_FIXTURE_TIME", "input", format!("seed row {index} ({table}): {why}")))?;
        if let Some(why) = wire::bare_principal(definition, &row) {
            return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("seed row {index} ({table}): {why}")));
        }
        match wire::row_from_json(definition, &row) {
            Ok(value) => typed.push((table.to_owned(), value)),
            Err(why) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{table}: {why}"))),
        }
    }
    Ok(typed)
}

fn seed_refusal(error: snapback4_core::RuntimeError) -> Refusal {
    let mut refusal = error.refusal();
    // Reseeding with changed immutable values reads like a rule problem; say what happened.
    if refusal.code == "E_CONSTRAINT" && refusal.message.ends_with(" is immutable") {
        refusal.message.push_str("; /seed is an upsert by id, and a row with that id already exists with a different value in that column. Reseed it with its original value, give the row a new id, POST /dev/reset first, or restart dev with --memory for an empty store");
    }
    refusal
}

/// What `POST /dev/reset` was asked to do: the body is empty, a `/seed`
/// array, or `{"seed": [..], "clock": "reset"}` with either key optional.
#[derive(Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
struct ResetRequest { seed: Vec<Json>, clock: bool }

fn reset_request(body: &str) -> Result<ResetRequest, Refusal> {
    const SHAPE: &str = "reset takes no body, a /seed array [{table, row}, ..], or {\"seed\": [..], \"clock\": \"reset\"} with either key optional";
    if body.trim().is_empty() { return Ok(ResetRequest::default()); }
    match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(Json::Array(seed)) => Ok(ResetRequest { seed, clock: false }),
        Ok(Json::Object(fields)) => {
            let mut request = ResetRequest::default();
            for (key, value) in fields {
                match (key.as_str(), value) {
                    ("seed", Json::Array(seed)) => request.seed = seed,
                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "reset" => request.clock = true,
                    ("clock", Json::String(clock)) if clock == "keep" => request.clock = false,
                    ("clock", other) => return Err(crate::dev_clock::input(format!("reset's clock is \"reset\" (back to wall time) or \"keep\" (the default), not {other}"))),
                    (key, _) => return Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{SHAPE}; got key {key:?}"))),
                }
            }
            Ok(request)
        }
        _ => Err(Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", SHAPE)),
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.25 — one transaction: empty, new identity, reseed; then the host's caches follow.
pub(super) fn reset(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.dev_doors() || !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|a| a.ip().is_loopback()) {
        respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&reset_release()) }));
        return;
    }
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    let refuse = |request, refusal: Refusal| respond_timed(request, shared, 400, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal.guide("testing")) }));
    let asked = match reset_request(body) {
        Ok(asked) => asked,
        Err(refusal) => return refuse(request, refusal),
    };
    // Serialize with clock advances, then take the engine before the clock:
    // the order clock advance and worker claims use.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    if let Err(why) = shared.dev_authority(&engine) { drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})); }
    let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock).clone();

//! Held change polls: JSON whitespace heartbeats, followed by one JSON result.
use super::{
    JsonBuffer, Shared, cors, lock, principal_of, query_params, refusal_json, respond_timed,
    trusted_dev_request,
};

const HEARTBEAT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);

pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, url: &str) {
    let params = query_params(url);
    let since: u64 = params.get("since").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    let wait: u64 = super::wait_seconds(url);
    let began = Instant::now();
    let deadline = began + Duration::from_secs(wait);
    let paced = began + Duration::from_millis(100);
    let capability = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
        Ok(principal) => principal,
        Err(refusal) => {
            return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
        }
    };
    if let Err(denied) = crate::device_handshake::check(shared, crate::device_handshake::header(&request), false) {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 409, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&denied) }));
    }
    let connected = &capability.0;
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#6-idle-live-clients-must-not-cost-a-thread — one principal's tabs cannot take the whole viewer pool; refused at once, never queued.
    let _share = if wait > 0 {
        match shared.long_waits.enter(connected) {
            Ok(share) => Some(share),
            Err(limit) => return super::long_waits::refuse_share(request, shared, limit),
        }
    } else { None };
    let present = capability.2 == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full;
    let heartbeat = params.get("heartbeat").is_some_and(|s| s == "1") && request.http_version().0 == 1 && request.http_version().1 >= 1;
    let mut response = PollResponse { request: Some(request), started: false, shared };
    let initial = { let wake=lock(&shared.wake); (wake.seq,wake.eph.version.clone(),wake.generation) };
    let epoch = params.get("eph").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(initial.1.epoch);
    let incarnation = params.get("ephi").cloned().unwrap_or(initial.1.incarnation);
    let interest: Option<std::collections::BTreeSet<String>> = params.get("ephTables").map(|s| {
        s.split(',').filter(|s|!s.is_empty()).map(str::to_owned).collect()
    });
    let wants_ephemeral = ["eph", "ephi", "ephTables"].iter().any(|k|params.contains_key(*k));
    {
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        if let Err(why) = super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(), shared, &engine) {
            drop(engine); response.finish(401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})); return;
        }
        if present && let Err(error) = engine.presence_reconnected(&connected, shared.now()) {
            response.finish(500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error.refusal()) })); return;
        }
        if present { shared.presence.enter(&connected); }
    }
    let result = (|| -> Result<_, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
        // Entry always reads the store: the client may already know a head
        // committed after the last publication (LLP 3000 finding 5).
        let mut current=read_answer(shared, since)?;
        let mut observed=initial.0;
        let mut sequence_wake=false;
        let mut beat=Instant::now();
        loop {
            // The deadline is private scheduling state. Production polls wake
            // at it even without another application request; development
            // clock jumps run the same advancement through their control
            // request and notify this condition variable.
            let opening = if shared.personas {
                None
            } else {
                shared.next_time_audience()?
            };
            // Snapshot and wait share one mutex. Commit cannot pass between
            // the predicate check and the condvar's atomic unlock-and-wait.
            let wake=lock(&shared.wake);
            if wake.seq != observed {
                observed=wake.seq; sequence_wake=true; drop(wake);
                current=answer(shared, since)?;
                continue;
            }
            let tables=wake.eph.changed(epoch,Some(&incarnation),interest.as_ref());
            let reset=wake.eph.version.incarnation!=incarnation;
            let eph_ready=reset||!tables.is_empty();
            let durable=wake.generation != initial.2 || sequence_wake || current.0!=since || params.get("store_id").is_some_and(|id|Some(id)!=current.1.as_ref());
            let now=Instant::now();
            if durable || now>=deadline || eph_ready && now>=paced {
                let eph=snapback4_core::engine::ephemeral::Change {preserved:wake.eph.preserved_since(epoch,&incarnation),epoch:wake.eph.version.epoch,incarnation:wake.eph.version.incarnation.clone(),tables};
                let generation=wake.generation;
                // Replacement publishes its identity with the incarnation. No
                // engine lock is needed to finish an ephemeral-only wake.
                if reset {current.1=wake.store_id.clone();}
                return Ok((current,generation,eph));
            }
            if heartbeat && now>=beat {
                drop(wake); response.heartbeat().map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
                beat=Instant::now()+HEARTBEAT;continue;
            }
            let mut until=if heartbeat {deadline.min(beat)} else {deadline};
            if eph_ready {until=until.min(paced);}
            if let Some(opening) = opening {
                until = until.min(Instant::now() + shared.time_audience_delay(opening));
            }
            #[cfg(test)]
            super::test_work(&shared.poll_wait);
            let (wake, timeout)=shared.polls.wait_timeout(wake,until.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now())).unwrap_or_else(|e|e.into_inner());
            #[cfg(test)]
            super::test_work(&shared.poll_resumed);
            #[cfg(test)]
            if !timeout.timed_out() { super::test_work(&shared.poll_notified); }
            drop(wake);
            if timeout.timed_out()
                && opening.is_some_and(|at| shared.time_audience_delay(at).is_zero())
            {
                shared.advance_time_audiences()?;
                current = answer(shared, since)?;
            }
        }
    })();
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — a reader never enters OR leaves presence.
    if present { shared.presence.leave(&connected); }
    // Recheck bearer liveness on a pooled detached reader. Ephemeral-only
    // wakes must not reacquire the serving engine merely to finish a poll.
    let auth_store_id = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
    let bearer = response.request.as_ref().unwrap().headers().iter().find(|h|h.field.equiv("Authorization")).and_then(|h|crate::auth::parse_bearer(h.value.as_str())).map(|token| token.trim().to_owned());
    let authenticated = match bearer.map(|token| (shared.reader(), token)) {
        None => Ok(()),
        Some((Ok((reader_epoch, reader)), token)) => {
            let resolved = match snapback4_core::identity::resolve_read_only_supervised(reader.store(),&token,snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now: shared.now(), supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire) },auth_store_id.as_deref()) {
                Ok(Some(current)) if current == capability => Ok(Ok(())),Ok(_)=>Ok(Err(snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(reader.store(), &token, shared.now(), trusted_dev_request(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(), shared)))),Err(e)=>Err(e),
            };
            match resolved {
                Ok(outcome) => { lock(&shared.readers).put(&reader_epoch, reader.into_inner()); outcome }
                // As for answers: a reader older than a finishing adoption is
                // not a verdict on the session. Ask again behind the engine.
                Err(_) => {
                    principal_of(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(),shared).and_then(|current| {
                        if current == capability { Ok(()) } else { Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the poll credential changed")) }
                    })
                }
            }
        }
        Some((Err(_), _)) => {
            principal_of(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(),shared).and_then(|current| {
                if current == capability { Ok(()) } else { Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the poll credential changed")) }
            })
        }
    };
    if let Err(refusal)=authenticated { response.finish(401,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)}));return; }
    match result {
        Ok(((seq,store_id,tables,preserved,changes),generation,ephemeral))=>{
            let mut reply=json!({"seq":seq,"tables":tables,"preserved":preserved,"changes":changes,"generation":generation,"store_id":store_id});
            if wants_ephemeral {reply["ephemeral"]=json!(ephemeral);}
            response.finish(200,reply);
        }
        Err(error)=>{let why=error.refusal();response.finish(if why.retryable {503}else{500},json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));}
    }
}

type Checked = (u64, Option<String>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>, Json);
// @ref LLP 3000.036#6-idle-live-clients-must-not-cost-a-thread — one commit wakes every held poll; they share one answer per cursor.
/// Wake answers by (cursor, published head, generation, store identity). Only
/// a held poll woken by a publication asks here, and an answer is computed
/// after reading that publication, so its snapshot is newer than the poll's
/// entry and than anything its client knew when it asked. The answer names
/// no viewer, so polls from the same cursor share one read: a commit costs one
/// read per distinct cursor, not one per held poll. Entry and unheld polls
/// read the store themselves (the published counter is a wake signal, never
/// the source of truth). A shared answer is at most a second old and never
/// below the cursor; failures are not kept.
#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Answers { entries: std::collections::VecDeque<((u64, u64, u64, Option<String>), Instant, Checked)> }
const ANSWERS_KEPT: usize = 64;
const ANSWER_AGE: Duration = Duration::from_secs(1);

fn answer(shared: &Shared, since: u64) -> Result<Checked, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
    let key = { let wake = lock(&shared.wake); (since, wake.seq, wake.generation, wake.store_id.clone()) };
    let mut answers = lock(&shared.change_answers);
    if let Some((_, _, hit)) = answers.entries.iter().find(|(k, at, hit)| *k == key && at.elapsed() < ANSWER_AGE && hit.0 >= since) {
        return Ok(hit.clone());
    }
    let checked = read_answer(shared, since)?;
    // Older publications are never asked for again.
    answers.entries.retain(|(k, at, _)| k.1 >= key.1 && *k != key && at.elapsed() < ANSWER_AGE);
    if answers.entries.len() >= ANSWERS_KEPT { answers.entries.pop_front(); }
    answers.entries.push_back((key, Instant::now(), checked.clone()));
    Ok(checked)
}

fn read_answer(shared: &Shared, since: u64) -> Result<Checked, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {

//! The online-only mutation route and a wake state independent of the writer.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::engine::ephemeral::{Hub, Snapshot};

pub(super) struct Wake {
    pub seq: u64,
    pub store_id: Option<String>,
    pub generation: u64,
    pub eph: Snapshot,
}
impl Wake {
    pub fn new(engine: &Engine) -> Self {
        Self {
            seq: engine.seq().unwrap_or(0),
            store_id: engine.store().meta("store_id").unwrap_or(None),
            generation: engine.generation(),
            eph: engine.ephemeral_hub().snapshot().expect("new hub"),
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Default)]
pub(super) struct Rate {
    buckets: Mutex<BTreeMap<String, (Instant, f64)>>,
    #[cfg(test)]
    fixed_now: Mutex<Option<Instant>>,
}
impl Rate {
    fn now(&self) -> Instant {
        #[cfg(test)]
        if let Some(now) = *lock(&self.fixed_now) {
            return now;
        }
        Instant::now()
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(super) fn set_now(&self, now: Instant) {
        *lock(&self.fixed_now) = Some(now);
    }
    pub fn admit(&self, key: String, now: Instant) -> bool {
        let mut buckets = lock(&self.buckets);
        buckets.retain(|_, (at, _)| now.saturating_duration_since(*at) < Duration::from_secs(60));
        if !buckets.contains_key(&key) && buckets.len() >= 4096 {
            return false;
        }
        let (at, tokens) = buckets.entry(key).or_insert((now, 40.0));
        *tokens = (*tokens + now.saturating_duration_since(*at).as_secs_f64() * 20.0).min(40.0);
        *at = now;
        if *tokens < 1.0 {
            false
        } else {
            *tokens -= 1.0;
            true
        }
    }
}
impl Shared {
    // @ref LLP 3000.017#3.3 — lock order engine → hub → wake.
    pub(super) fn install_ephemeral_wake(self: &Arc<Self>) {
        let owner = Arc::downgrade(self);
        self.ephemeral.set_publisher(move |snapshot| {
            if let Some(shared) = owner.upgrade() {
                let mut wake = lock(&shared.wake);
                wake.store_id = Some(snapshot.identity.clone());
                wake.generation = snapshot.generation;
                wake.eph = snapshot;
                drop(wake);
                shared.polls.notify_all();
            }
        }).expect("new hub publisher");
    }
    pub(super) fn durable_committed(&self, engine: &Engine) {
        self.publish_durable(engine, true);
    }
    pub(super) fn publish_engine(&self, engine: &Engine) {
        self.publish_durable(engine, false);
    }
    fn publish_durable(&self, engine: &Engine, committed: bool) {
        if let Ok(seq) = engine.seq() {
            let store_id = engine.store().meta("store_id").unwrap_or(None);
            let generation = engine.generation();
            let mut current = lock(&self.seq);
            let mut wake = lock(&self.wake);
            let changed = wake.seq != seq || wake.store_id != store_id || wake.generation != generation;
            *current = seq;
            wake.seq = seq;
            wake.store_id = store_id;
            wake.generation = generation;
            // The hub publisher alone owns wake.eph. Never wait for a running
            // ephemeral program while holding the serving engine here.
            if committed || changed {
                self.polls.notify_all();
                self.committed.notify_all();
            }
        }
    }
}

pub(super) fn failed(request: Request, shared: &Shared, id: Option<&str>, why: Refusal) {
    let wake = lock(&shared.wake);
    let (store_id, head) = (wake.store_id.clone(), wake.seq);
    drop(wake);
    respond_timed(
        request,
        shared,
        200,
        super::mutation_reply(json!({"state":"failed","id":id,"why":refusal_json(&why),"store_id":store_id,"head":head})),
    );
}

                ),

    // A name may have been durable in a previous generation. Receipt custody
    // precedes signature decoding, rate admission and ephemeral execution.
    // @ref LLP 3000.022#6 — a stored success never executes a replacement body.
    let fingerprint = match delivery_fingerprint(&payload) {
        Ok(value) => value,
        Err(why) => return failed(request, why),
    };
    {
        let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
        let preflight = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal)
            .and_then(|()| intent_store(&engine, &payload))
            .and_then(|()| {
                let args = payload.get("args").cloned().unwrap_or(json!({}));
                id.as_deref().map(|id| engine.replay_mutation(&principal, id, op, &args))
                    .transpose().map(Option::flatten).map_err(|e| e.refusal())
            });
        match preflight {
            Ok(Some(out)) => {
                let (store_id, head) = match response_head(&mut engine) {
                    Ok(value) => value,
                    Err(why) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied":why})),
                };
                let mut reply = out.reply(id.as_deref());
                reply["store_id"] = json!(store_id);
                reply["head"] = json!(head);
                return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply);
            }
            Err(why) => return failed(request, why),
            Ok(None) => {
                if let Err(error)=engine.require_non_media_ephemeral(&principal,id.as_deref()) { return failed(request,error.refusal()); }
                // @ref LLP 3000#http-overload-diagnostics — an ID alone does not deduplicate ephemeral writes, including after schema adoption.
                if payload.get("retry_write").and_then(Json::as_bool) == Some(true) {
                    return failed(request, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "retry_write requires a durable mutation; refresh the operation signature"));
                }
                if let Err(error) = engine.schema().check_delivery(op, fingerprint.as_deref()) {
                    return failed(request, error.refusal());
                }
            }
        }
    }
    // Bearer digests identify sessions without retaining secrets. Dev personas
    // have no session token and each principal is their development session.
    let key = crate::auth::bearer_token(&request)
        .map(|token| snapback4_core::value::digest(&token))
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| format!("persona:{principal}"));
    if !shared.ephemeral_rate.admit(key, shared.ephemeral_rate.now()) {
        let mut why = Refusal::new(
            "E_RATE_LIMIT",
            "auth",
            "ephemeral writes allow 20 requests per second, burst 40; retry later",
        )
        .guide("ephemeral");
        why.retryable = true;
        return failed(request, why);
    }
    let signature = lock(&shared.bound)
        .programs
        .iter()
        .find(|p| p.program.name == op)
        .map(|p| p.program.args.clone());
    let Some(signature) = signature else {
        return failed(
            request,
            Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("unknown operation {op}")),
        );
    };
    let empty = json!({});
    let args_json = payload.get("args").unwrap_or(&empty);
    if let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&signature,args_json) {return failed(request,why);}
    let Some(fields) = args_json.as_object() else {
        return failed(
            request,
            Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "args must be an object"),
        );
    };
    let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
    for (name, value) in fields {
        let Some(kind) = signature.get(name) else {
            return failed(
                request,
                Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{op} takes no argument {name}")),
            );
        };
        match wire::from_json(kind, value) {
            Ok(value) => {
                args.insert(name.clone(), value);
            }
            Err(why) => {
                return failed(
                    request,
                    Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("argument {name}: {why}")),
                );
            }
        }
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    test_work(&shared.mutation_wait);
    let (reader, sites) = {
        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — ephemeral writes also lose authority while queued for adoption.
        if let Err(why) = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal) {
            return failed(request, why);
        }
        (shared.take_reader(&engine), shared.personas.then(|| Arc::new(lock(&shared.bound).refusal_sites.clone())))
    };
    let mut context = Context::new(principal.clone(), args).at(shared.now());
    context.delivery_fingerprint = fingerprint;
    let result = match reader {
        Ok((epoch, mut reader)) => {
            if let Some(sites) = sites { context = super::dev_refusals::response::context(context, sites, &reader); }
            let _timing = latency::EngineSpan::new();
            let result = intent_store(&reader, &payload).and_then(|()| {
                reader.require_non_media_ephemeral(&principal,id.as_deref()).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?;
                reader
                    .mutate_ephemeral(op, context)
                    .map_err(|e| snapback4_core::engine::seat_refusal(reader.schema(),&e,&principal))
            });
            let store_id = reader.store().meta("store_id").ok().flatten();
            if result.is_ok() {
                lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader);
            }
            result.map(|out| (out, store_id))
        }
        // In-memory test stores have no detached SQLite connection.
        Err(_) => {
            #[cfg(test)]
            test_work(&shared.mutation_wait);
            let mut engine = producing_engine(shared);
            if shared.personas { context = super::dev_refusals::response::context(context, Arc::new(lock(&shared.bound).refusal_sites.clone()), &engine); }
            mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal)
                .and_then(|()| intent_store(&engine, &payload))
                .and_then(|()| {
                    engine.require_non_media_ephemeral(&principal,id.as_deref()).map_err(|e|e.refusal())?;
                    engine
                        .mutate_ephemeral(op, context)
                        .map_err(|e| snapback4_core::engine::seat_refusal(engine.schema(),&e,&principal))
                })
                .map(|out| (out, engine.store().meta("store_id").ok().flatten()))
        }
    };
    match result {
        Ok((out, store_id)) => {
            respond_timed(
                request,
                shared,
                200,
                super::mutation_reply(json!({"state":"sent","id":id,"result":wire::to_json(&out.result),"ephemeral":out.ephemeral,"store_id":store_id,"head":out.seq})),
            );
        }
        Err(why) => failed(request, why),
    }
}

pub(super) fn hub(engine: &Engine) -> Arc<Hub> {
    engine.ephemeral_hub()
}

/// Ordinary handwritten calls keep their spelling; SDK reports name the binding.
pub(super) fn delivery_fingerprint(payload: &Json) -> Result<Option<String>, Refusal> {
    let Some(delivery) = payload.get("delivery") else { return Ok(None) };
    delivery.get("fingerprint").and_then(Json::as_str).filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
        .map(|s| Some(s.to_owned())).ok_or_else(|| {
            let mut why = Refusal::new("E_DELIVERY_BINDING", "schema", "delivery needs the fingerprint collected from its binding").guide("receipts");
            why.site = Some("delivery.fingerprint".into());
            why.rewrite = Some("send delivery: { fingerprint } from the manifest or compiled binding".into());

//! Authenticated, bounded controller transport. Custody is per consumer, never
//! an operation-global cursor shared by tabs. Bodies contain no work counters.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::engine::following_history::{Request as UnitRequest, projection};

pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, operation: &str, body: &str) {
    if body.len() > 64 * 1024 {
        return respond_timed(
            request,
            shared,
            400,
            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
        );
    }
    let (principal, _, scope) = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(r) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&r)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — Following custody is outside the closed read allow-list.
    // The outer guard refuses before body intake; retain the boundary here too.
    if scope == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read {
        let why = Refusal::new(
            "E_READ_ONLY",
            "auth",
            "Following history requires a full session; ordinary inbox queries remain available",
        )
        .guide("read-only");
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
    }
    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) {
        Ok(v) => v,
        Err(_) => {
            return respond_timed(
                request,
                shared,
                400,
                json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
            );
        }
    };
    // Lock order is engine then controller. Authentication is checked again at
    // the same engine incarnation that owns the source read transaction.
    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
    if let Err(r) = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal) {
        drop(engine);
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&r)}));
    }
    let Some(program) = engine.program(operation) else {
        drop(engine);
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"phase":"restart"}));
    };
    let Some(plan) = projection::plan(engine.schema(), program) else {
        drop(engine);
        return respond_timed(
            request,
            shared,
            200,
            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_DECLARATION"}),
        );
    };
    let binding = plan.binding;
    let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
    let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(Json::as_object) else {
        drop(engine);
        return respond_timed(
            request,
            shared,
            400,
            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
        );
    };
    if let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,&Json::Object(fields.clone())) {
        drop(engine);return respond_timed(request,shared,400,json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT","message":why.message,"denied":why}));
    }
    for (name, value) in fields {
        let Some(value) = program
            .args
            .get(name)
            .and_then(|kind| wire::from_json(kind, value).ok())
        else {
            drop(engine);
            return respond_timed(
                request,
                shared,
                400,
                json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
            );
        };
        args.insert(name.clone(), value);
    }
    let now = shared.now();
    let mut reader = lock(&shared.following);
    let action = payload
        .get("action")
        .and_then(Json::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("unit");
    let answer = if action == "open" {
        reader
            .opening_projected(&engine, operation, Context::new(&principal, args).at(now))
            .map(|o| json!({"opening":o.request.opening}).to_string())
    } else if let Some(opening) = payload.get("opening").and_then(Json::as_str) {
        if action == "cancel" {
            reader
                .cancel_projected(
                    &mut engine,
                    opening,
                    operation,
                    Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
                )
                .map(|_| json!({"phase":"restart"}).to_string())
        } else if action == "refresh" {
            let mounted = serde_json::from_value::<
                Vec<snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Mounted>,
            >(payload.get("mounted").cloned().unwrap_or(json!([])));
            let seen = serde_json::from_value::<Vec<String>>(
                payload.get("seen").cloned().unwrap_or(json!([])),
            );
            match (mounted, seen) {
                (Ok(mounted), Ok(seen)) => reader
                    .refresh_slice_metered(
                        &mut engine,
                        opening,
                        operation,
                        Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
                        &mounted,
                        &seen,
                    )
                    .map(|(r, _)| serde_json::to_string(&r).unwrap()),
                _ => Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string()),
            }
        } else if action == "unit"
            && payload
                .get("token")
                .is_some_and(|v| !v.is_null() && !v.is_string())
        {
            Ok(json!({"phase":"restart"}).to_string())
        } else if action == "unit" {
            let q = UnitRequest {
                declaration: binding.declaration,
                page_size: binding.page_size,
                opening: opening.into(),
                token: payload
                    .get("token")
                    .and_then(Json::as_str)
                    .map(str::to_owned),
            };
            reader
                .request_projected(
                    &mut engine,
                    q,
                    Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
                    operation,
                )
                .map(|out| out.answer.bytes().to_owned())
        } else {
            Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string())
        }
    } else {
        Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string())
    };
    drop(reader);
    drop(engine);
    let bytes =
        answer.unwrap_or_else(|e| json!({"phase":"refused","code":e.refusal().code}).to_string());

fn is_claim(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
    method == &Method::Post && (path == "/effects/claim" || path == "/actions/claim")
}

/// `/changes`' own reading of `wait` (absent or unparsable is 0; capped at 25).
pub(crate) fn wait_seconds(url: &str) -> u64 {
    query_params(url).get("wait").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0).min(25)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#93-http-admission-and-reporting — stamp actual engine work after buffered intake, without pinning worker waits or socket I/O.
pub(crate) fn producing_engine(shared:&Shared) -> engine_lock::FairGuard<'_,Engine> {
    let engine=lock(&shared.engine);
    crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
    engine
}

/// A worker's seat: a bearer session of a service principal.
fn service_of(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<String, Refusal> {
    let engine=lock(&shared.engine);
    let result=service_of_locked(request,shared,&engine);
    if result.is_err() {crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());}
    result
}

fn service_of_locked(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine) -> Result<String, Refusal> {
    dev_request(request, shared)?;
    let refused = || Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "this is a worker's door: send a live service token (`snapback4 token --service <name>`)").guide("effects");
    if !matches!(single_header(request, "x-snapback-persona"), Ok(None)) { return Err(refused()); }
    let token = crate::auth::bearer_token(request).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
    snapback4_core::identity::current_service(engine.store(), &token, snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample {
        now: shared.now(), supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire),
    }).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?.ok_or_else(refused)
}

pub(crate) fn single_header<'a>(request: &'a Request, name: &str) -> Result<Option<&'a str>, ()> {
    let mut headers = request.headers().iter().filter(|h| h.field.as_str().as_str().eq_ignore_ascii_case(name));
    let value = headers.next().map(|h| h.value.as_str());
    if headers.next().is_some() { Err(()) } else { Ok(value) }
}

fn authority_host(authority: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    if authority.is_empty() || authority.chars().any(|c| c.is_ascii_whitespace() || "/\\?#@,".contains(c)) { return None; }
    if let Some(rest) = authority.strip_prefix('[') {
        let (host, tail) = rest.split_once(']')?;
        if !matches!(host.parse::<IpAddr>().ok(), Some(IpAddr::V6(_))) { return None; }
        if !tail.is_empty() { tail.strip_prefix(':')?.parse::<u16>().ok().filter(|p| *p > 0)?; }
        Some(host)
    } else if let Some((host, port)) = authority.split_once(':') {
        port.parse::<u16>().ok().filter(|p| *p > 0)?;
        if host.is_empty() { None } else { Some(host) }
    } else { Some(authority) }
}

fn local_authority(authority: &str) -> bool {
    authority_host(authority).is_some_and(|host| host.eq_ignore_ascii_case("localhost") || host.parse::<IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback()))
}

fn origin_authority(origin: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    let authority = origin.strip_prefix("http://").or_else(|| origin.strip_prefix("https://"))?;
    authority_host(authority)?;
    Some(authority)
}

/// Browser developers use different local app ports. Trust literal loopback
/// names, never DNS resolution: a foreign hostname may resolve to loopback.
fn dev_request(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    if !shared.personas { return Ok(()); }
    let host = single_header(request, "host");
    let origin = single_header(request, "origin");
    if !request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|addr| addr.ip().is_loopback())
        || !request.url().starts_with('/') || request.url().starts_with("//")
        || !matches!(host, Ok(Some(host)) if local_authority(host))
        || !matches!(origin, Ok(None) | Ok(Some(_)))
        || origin.ok().flatten().is_some_and(|origin| !origin_authority(origin).is_some_and(local_authority)) {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "development HTTP requires a loopback Host and a loopback browser Origin; use localhost or a literal loopback address").guide("personas"));
    }
    Ok(())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — public routes bypass dev admission, never the privacy boundary.
pub(crate) fn trusted_dev_request(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> bool {
    shared.personas && dev_request(request, shared).is_ok()
}

pub(crate) fn cors(request: &Request) -> Vec<Header> {
    let mut headers = vec![
        Header::from_bytes("Vary", "Origin").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "X-Snapback-Time, X-Snapback-Clock, X-Snapback-Generation").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "content-type, x-snapback-persona, x-snapback-meter, authorization, range, x-snapback-device").unwrap(),
        Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS").unwrap(),
    ];
    if let Ok(Some(origin)) = single_header(request, "origin") && origin_authority(origin).is_some() {
        headers.push(Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin).unwrap());
    }
    if latency::headers_enabled() {
        headers.push(Header::from_bytes("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "Server-Timing").unwrap());
    }
    headers
}

const SYNC_REQUEST_CEILING: usize = 256 << 10;

                        trace::mark("query_end");
                        let scopes = read_scopes::complete(scopes, reader.schema(), outcome.as_ref().map_or(&[], |o| &o.tables));
                        if outcome.is_ok() { lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner()); }
                        (outcome, cut, metadata, reads_presence, query_generation, current, scopes, decoration_paths)
                    },
                    // Only memory stores lack detached readers by construction.
                    Err(error) if !shared.memory_store() => {
                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied": error.refusal()}));
                    }
                    Err(_) => {
                        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
                        if let Err(why) = session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &engine) {
                            drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
                        }
                        let metadata = response_head(&mut engine);
                        let reads_presence = presence_of(&engine);
                        let mut context = context.clone();
                        if shared.personas { context = dev_refusals::response::context(context, Arc::new(lock(&shared.bound).refusal_sites.clone()), &engine); }
                        let query_generation = engine.generation();
                        let decoration_paths = engine.program(&op).filter(|_| engine.schema().operations.get(&op).is_some_and(|f| f.search_decoration)).map(snapback4_core::search_decoration::paths);
                        let scopes = read_scopes::dependencies(&engine, &op, &context);
                        let (outcome, cut, current) = engine.query_materialized_search_with_delivery_at(&op, context, retained.as_deref(), manifest);
                        let scopes = read_scopes::complete(scopes, engine.schema(), outcome.as_ref().map_or(&[], |o| &o.tables));
                        (outcome, cut, metadata, reads_presence, query_generation, current, scopes, decoration_paths)
                    },
                };
                if let Some((table, id)) = evaluated.0.as_ref().ok().and_then(|outcome| outcome.pending_opening.as_ref()) {
                    if settlement_started.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(30) || crate::lifecycle::stopping() {
                        let mut why = Refusal::new("E_QUERY_RETRY", "client", "timed disclosure did not settle within the query wait; retry the query").guide("client");
                        why.retryable = true;
                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied":why}));
                    }
                    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
                    let now = match engine.materialize_time_audience_at(table, id, context.now.unwrap_or_else(|| shared.now())) {
                        Ok(now) => now,
                        Err(error) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied":error.refusal()})),
                    };
                    shared.refresh_time_audience_clock(&engine, now);
                    shared.durable_committed(&engine);
                    drop(engine);
                    if let Err(why) = session_identity::principal_of(&request, shared) {
                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":why}));
                    }
                    std::thread::yield_now();
                    continue;
                }
                break evaluated;
                };
                let (store_id, head) = match metadata { Ok(head) => head, Err(why) => { respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": why })); return; } };
                match outcome {
                    Ok(outcome) => {
                        #[derive(serde::Serialize)]
                        struct QueryReply<'a> {
                            #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            scopes: &'a Option<Json>,
                            #[serde(rename = "searchScopes", skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            search_scopes: &'a Option<Vec<String>>,
                            #[serde(rename = "searchRows", skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            search_rows: Option<wire::Plain<'a>>,
                            #[serde(rename = "searchCurrent", skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            search_current: &'a Option<Vec<String>>,
                            #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            delivery: &'a Option<snapback4_core::engine::delivery::Manifest>,
                            #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            ephemeral: &'a Option<snapback4_core::schema::ephemeral::Version>,
                            generation: u64,
                            complete: bool,
                            cut: Option<snapback4_core::engine::ReadCut>,
                            data: wire::Plain<'a>,
                            next: &'a Option<String>,
                            #[serde(rename = "nextExpiry")]
                            next_expiry: Option<i64>,
                            #[serde(rename = "decorationPaths", skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            decoration_paths: Option<Vec<Vec<String>>>,
                            #[serde(rename = "decorationExpiry", skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            decoration_expiry: Option<Option<i64>>,
                            seq: u64,
                            store_id: String,
                            tables: &'a [String],
                            viewer: &'a str,
                            #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            meter: Option<Json>,
                            #[serde(rename = "rowProvenance", skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            row_provenance: Option<wire::Plain<'a>>,
                            #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
                            refresh: Option<u64>,
                        }
                        impl latency::Reply for QueryReply<'_> { fn generation(&self)->Option<u64>{Some(self.generation)} }
                        // @ref LLP 3000.018#3 — an answer of a query that may read presence asks its client to re-run it: no change feed names presence.
                        let refresh = reads_presence.then(|| PRESENCE_REFRESH.as_millis() as u64);
                        // @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — loopback dev returns read counters by default; releases never disclose them.
                        let meter = shared.personas.then(|| {
                            if let Some(line) = lock(&shared.read_cuts).line(&op, &viewer, &outcome, Instant::now()) { snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{line}"); }
                            let m = outcome.metered;
                            let cut = if outcome.capped_at.is_some() { Some("meter") } else if outcome.word_cut_at.is_some() { Some("words") } else { None };
                            let mut meter = json!({ "examined": m.examined, "indexEntries": m.index_entries, "probes": m.rule_probes, "steps": m.steps, "heapBytes": m.heap_bytes, "outputBytes": m.output_bytes, "rowsWritten": m.rows_written, "cut": cut });
                            if let Some(delivery) = &outcome.delivery_meter { meter["delivery"] = json!(delivery); }
                            meter
                        });
                        if outcome.unavailable {
                            let mut reply = json!({ "loading": true, "generation": query_generation, "data": null, "complete": false, "nextExpiry": outcome.next_expiry, "seq": outcome.seq, "store_id": store_id, "tables": outcome.tables, "viewer": viewer });
                            if let Some(version) = &outcome.ephemeral { reply["ephemeral"] = json!(version); }
                            if let Some(meter) = meter { reply["meter"] = meter; }
                            if let Some(refresh) = refresh { reply["refresh"] = json!(refresh); }
                            respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply);
                        } else {
                            let row_provenance = provenance.then(|| outcome.data.retained_rows_mask());
                            respond_timed(request, shared, 200, QueryReply { scopes: &scopes, search_current: &search_current, generation: query_generation, search_scopes: &outcome.search_scopes, search_rows: outcome.search_rows.as_ref().map(wire::Plain), delivery: &outcome.delivery, ephemeral: &outcome.ephemeral, row_provenance: row_provenance.as_ref().map(wire::Plain), complete: outcome.complete, cut: outcome.cut, data: wire::Plain(&outcome.data), next: &outcome.next, next_expiry: outcome.next_expiry, decoration_expiry: decoration_paths.is_some().then_some(outcome.decoration_expiry), decoration_paths, seq: outcome.seq, store_id, tables: &outcome.tables, viewer: &viewer, meter, refresh });
                        }
                    }
                    Err(refusal) => {
                        let mut reply = json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal), "generation": query_generation, "complete": false, "store_id": store_id, "head": head });
                        // @ref LLP 3000.018#3.2 — a refusal names the cut its evaluation read, which the head
                        // taken before it may predate, so a client can order it against a remembered answer.
                        if let Some(cut) = cut { reply["seq"] = json!(cut); }
                        // A refusal can depend on presence too: its watch keeps refreshing (LLP 3000.018 §3.2).
                        if reads_presence && !refusal.retryable { reply["refresh"] = json!(PRESENCE_REFRESH.as_millis() as u64); }
                        respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if refusal.code=="E_INPUT" && refusal.family=="input" {400} else {200}, reply);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                if kind == ProgramKind::Query {
                    operation_refusal(request, shared, 400, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{op} is a query; POST /q/{op}")));
                    return;
                }
                if kind == ProgramKind::Job {
                    operation_refusal(request, shared, 400, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{op} is a job: a schedule fires it, or `snapback4 run {op}` does (in dev also POST /dev/jobs/{op} with {{\"args\":{{..}}}}); no client call can")).guide("jobs"));
                    return;
                }
                let id = payload.get("id").and_then(Json::as_str).map(str::to_owned);
                if let Some(ids) = payload.get("newIds").and_then(Json::as_array) {
                    context = context.with_ids(ids.iter().filter_map(Json::as_str).map(str::to_owned));
                }
                #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.mutation_wait);
                let (outcome, metadata, preserved, generation) = {
                    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
                    if let Err(refusal) = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal) {
                        drop(engine);
                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)}));
                    }
                    if engine.schema().operations.get(&op).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral) {
                        drop(engine);
                        return ephemeral::serve(request, shared, &op, &body);
                    }
                    if shared.personas { context = dev_refusals::response::context(context, Arc::new(lock(&shared.bound).refusal_sites.clone()), &engine); }
                    context.delivery_fingerprint = delivery_fingerprint;
                    let outcome = intent_store(&engine, &payload).and_then(|()| engine.mutate(&op, id.as_deref(), context.with_wire_args(args_json)).map_err(|error| error.refusal()));

//! HTTP identity admission. Password work owns a bounded KDF slot, never
//! the engine guard. Rate keys use the socket peer, never forwarded headers.
use crate::serve::{Shared, principal_of, refusal_json, respond_timed};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
use snapback4_core::{RuntimeError, identity};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{
    Mutex,
    atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering},
};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tiny_http::Request;

pub const BODY_CEILING: usize = 4096;
const WINDOW: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);
const KEYS: usize = 4096;

pub struct Admission {
    attempts: Mutex<HashMap<String, (Instant, u32)>>,
    kdfs: AtomicUsize,
    dev_kdf_slots: usize,
}
impl Default for Admission {
    fn default() -> Self {
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#14 — ruling 9: dev loopback KDF work fits the host, bounded at 2–16 slots
        let dev_kdf_slots = std::thread::available_parallelism().map(|n| n.get().clamp(2, 16)).unwrap_or(2);
        Self { attempts: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()), kdfs: AtomicUsize::new(0), dev_kdf_slots }
    }
}
impl Admission {
    pub fn kdf_slots(&self, dev_loopback: bool) -> usize {
        if dev_loopback { self.dev_kdf_slots } else { 2 }
    }

    fn attempt(&self, peer: &str, email: Option<&str>, now: Instant) -> bool {
        let mut attempts = self.attempts.lock().unwrap();
        attempts.retain(|_, (start, _)| now.duration_since(*start) < WINDOW);
        let mut keys = vec![(format!("ip:{peer}"), 30)];
        if let Some(email) = email {
            keys.push((format!("email:{}", identity::normalized_email(email)), 10));
        }
        // Charge every attempt, including denials. A full table refuses new
        // keys until expiry rather than evicting a key under attack.
        let mut admitted = true;
        for (key, limit) in keys {
            // A peer that is already over budget may charge existing email
            // keys, but cannot fill the shared map by rotating fresh emails.
            if !admitted && !attempts.contains_key(&key) {
                continue;
            }
            if !attempts.contains_key(&key) && attempts.len() >= KEYS {
                admitted = false;
                continue;
            }
            let (_, count) = attempts.entry(key).or_insert((now, 0));
            *count = count.saturating_add(1);
            admitted &= *count <= limit;
        }
        admitted
    }
    fn kdf(&self, dev_loopback: bool) -> Option<Kdf<'_>> {
        let slots = self.kdf_slots(dev_loopback);
        self.kdfs
            .fetch_update(Ordering::AcqRel, Ordering::Acquire, |n| {
                (n < slots).then_some(n + 1)
            })
            .ok()?;
        Some(Kdf(&self.kdfs))
    }
}
struct Kdf<'a>(&'a AtomicUsize);
impl Drop for Kdf<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.0.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::Release);
    }
}
fn limited(request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    let mut refusal = Refusal::new(
        "E_RATE_LIMIT",
        "auth",
        "too many identity requests; retry later",
    )
    .guide("personas");
    refusal.retryable = true;
    respond_timed(request, shared, 429, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
}

pub fn handle(request: Request, shared: &Shared, path: &str, body: &str) {
    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(if body.is_empty() { "{}" } else { body }) {
        Ok(value) => value,
        Err(_) => {
            return respond_timed(request, shared,
                400,
                json!({ "denied": Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "identity takes a JSON object") }),
            );
        }
    };
    let email = payload.get("email").and_then(Json::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    let password = payload.get("password").and_then(Json::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    let password_route = matches!(path, "/auth/login" | "/auth/signup");
    let peer = request
        .remote_addr()
        .map(|a| a.ip().to_string())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".into());
    let rate_route = matches!(path, "/auth/login" | "/auth/signup" | "/auth/guest");
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — dev loopback has no minute counters; KDF slots remain
    let dev_loopback = shared.personas && request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback());
    if rate_route && !dev_loopback
        && !shared
            .auth
            .attempt(&peer, password_route.then_some(email), Instant::now())
    {
        return limited(request, shared);
    }
    let kdf = if password_route {
        match shared.auth.kdf(dev_loopback) {
            Some(permit) => Some(permit),
            None => return limited(request, shared),
        }
    } else {
        None
    };
    let scope = match payload.get("scope") {
        None => Ok(identity::SessionScope::Full),
        Some(Json::String(value)) if value == "full" => Ok(identity::SessionScope::Full),
        Some(Json::String(value)) if value == "read" => Ok(identity::SessionScope::Read),
        _ => Err(RuntimeError::Input("scope must be full or read".into())),
    };

/bin/bash -lc 'timeout 10s cat snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/serve_token.rs' in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-b16-stage
 succeeded in 0ms:
//! The CLI's local service-seat door. This capability never comes from a bearer session.
use super::{Shared, single_header, authority_host, producing_engine, respond_with_cors};
use snapback4_core::{error::Refusal, identity::{self, Session}};
use std::{io::{self, Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write}, net::TcpStream, path::{Path, PathBuf}, time::Duration};
use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
use tiny_http::{Method, Request};

pub(crate) const ENDPOINT: &str = "/dev/token";
#[path = "serve_token_protocol.rs"]
mod protocol;
use protocol::{Mint, CLIENT, SERVER, PROOF};
pub(crate) use protocol::Authority;
const BODY_LIMIT: usize = 4096;
const SECRET_LEN: usize = 64;

fn secret_path(store: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    let mut path = store.as_os_str().to_owned();
    path.push("-token");
    path.into()
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — preserve the hardened auxiliary-file checks and the single owner's lifetime.
pub(crate) fn publish(store: &Path) -> io::Result<Authority> {
    #[cfg(unix)] {
        let secret = protocol::hex(&protocol::random()?);
        let mut file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), true, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
        file.set_len(0)?;
        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))?;
        file.write_all(secret.as_bytes())?;
        Ok(Authority::new(secret, protocol::store_identity(store)?))
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
        let _ = store;
        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    }
}

fn read_secret(store: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    #[cfg(unix)] {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), false, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
        if file.metadata()?.permissions().mode() & 0o777 != 0o600 {
            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof must have mode 0600"));
        }
        let mut secret = String::new();
        file.take((SECRET_LEN + 1) as u64).read_to_string(&mut secret)?;
        if secret.len() != SECRET_LEN || !secret.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof is incomplete"));
        }
        Ok(secret)
    }
    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
        let _ = store;
        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    }
}

fn refusal(code: &str, family: &str, message: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Refusal {
    Refusal::new(code, family, message).site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(rewrite).guide("services")
}

pub(super) fn denied(request: Request) {
    respond_with_cors(request, 403, json!({"denied": refusal("E_AUTH", "auth",
        "service token minting requires a loopback peer and this server's private store proof",
        "run snapback4 token --service <name> on the server host as the store owner (add --release <dir> for serve)")}), false);
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — authenticate both ends before minting; the store key never leaves its host file/memory.
pub(super) fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    let local = request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback())
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "host"), Ok(Some(host)) if authority_host(host)
            .is_some_and(|host| host.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback())))
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "origin"), Ok(None))
        && matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-store-secret"), Ok(None));
    let Some(authority) = shared.owner.as_ref().and_then(|o| o.token_authority.as_ref()).filter(|_| local) else {
        return denied(request);
    };
    if request.method() != &Method::Post || request.url() != ENDPOINT
        || request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_LIMIT) { return denied(request); }
    let client = match single_header(&request, CLIENT) {
        Ok(Some(value)) => match protocol::nonce(value) { Some(nonce) => nonce, None => return denied(request) },
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let (server, proof) = match (single_header(&request, SERVER), single_header(&request, PROOF)) {
        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
        },
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
        return denied(request);
    }
    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
        _ => return denied(request),
    };
    let binding = mint.binding();
    let Some(server) = server else {
        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
    };
    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
        return denied(request);
    }
    let result = {
        // Use the same writer and identity routine as offline minting. Sample
        // wall time as the offline CLI does, independent of dev's test clock.
        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, snapback4_core::now_ms(), mint.days)
        })
    };
    let reply = match result {
        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
        Err(error) => {
            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
            json!({"denied": why})
        }
    };
    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
        return denied(request);
    };
    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
}

fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)))?;
    // HTTP/1.0 keeps the bounded reply length-delimited. No proxy, DNS or redirects.
    write!(stream, "POST {ENDPOINT} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {address}\r\n{headers}Content-Type: application/json\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len())?;
    let mut response = String::new();
    stream.take(8193).read_to_string(&mut response)?;
    response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").filter(|_| response.len() <= 8192
        && (response.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 200 ") || response.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 ")))
        .and_then(|(_, body)| serde_json::from_str(body).ok())
        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("invalid local mint reply"))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — busy token commands ask the announced loopback owner, never open a second writer.
pub(crate) fn mint_live(path: &Path, name: &str, days: i64) -> Result<Session, (i32, String)> {
    let store = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_owned());
    let owner = crate::store_owner::address(&store).unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
    let unavailable = |reason: &str| (2, format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: owned by {owner}; {reason}. Restore access to that server's loopback listener and private store proof, then rerun `snapback4 token --service <name>` (with the same --release <dir> for serve). For an older or unreachable server, stop it, rerun token offline, then restart it; for another CLI, wait for it to finish. Leave <database>-owner in place", path.display()));
    let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(&store).ok_or_else(|| unavailable("no published loopback listener; no mint request sent"))?;
    let secret = read_secret(&store).map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot read the 0600 store token proof; no mint request sent"))?;
    let mint = Mint { service: name.into(), days, store: protocol::store_identity(&store)
        .map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot identify the owned store; no mint request sent"))? };
    let body = serde_json::to_string(&mint).unwrap();
    if body.len() > BODY_LIMIT { return Err((1, "token: E_AUTH at --service: a service name is 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -; use --service worker".into())); }
    let binding = mint.binding();
    let client = protocol::random().map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot create a fresh challenge; no mint request sent"))?;
    let headers = format!("{CLIENT}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&client));
    let challenge = exchange(address, &headers, &body).map_err(|_| unavailable("server challenge unavailable; no mint proof sent"))?;
    let server = challenge["server_nonce"].as_str().and_then(protocol::nonce)
        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("invalid server challenge; no mint proof sent"))?;
    if !challenge["server_proof"].as_str().is_some_and(|proof| protocol::verify_server(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, proof)) {
        return Err(unavailable("server proof did not verify; no mint proof sent"));
    }
    // Only a verified server receives a client proof. Neither message contains the key.
    let headers = format!("{headers}{SERVER}: {}\r\n{PROOF}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&server),
        protocol::client_proof(&secret, &client, &server, &binding));
    let reply = exchange(address, &headers, &body)
        .map_err(|_| unavailable("mint reply unavailable; a session may have been minted, but no token was received"))?;
    let reply: Json = reply["sealed"].as_str().and_then(|sealed| protocol::open(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, sealed))
        .and_then(|bytes| serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).ok())
        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"))?;
    if let Some(denied) = reply.get("denied") {
        if let Ok(why) = serde_json::from_value::<Refusal>(denied.clone()) {
            return Err((1, format!("token: {} at {ENDPOINT}: {}; {}", why.code, why.message, why.rewrite.unwrap_or_else(|| "rerun snapback4 token with valid service arguments".into()))));
        }
        return Err(unavailable("server refused the local mint request; restart it with this CLI and retry"));
    }
    let session = &reply["session"];
    let principal = format!("service:{name}");
    let token = session["token"].as_str().filter(|t| t.len() == 43 && t.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"_-".contains(&c)));
    if session["principal"] != principal || session["kind"] != "service" || token.is_none() || session["expires_at"].as_i64().is_none() {
        return Err(unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"));
    }
    Ok(Session { scope: snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full, cap_expires_at: None, principal, kind: "service".into(), token: token.unwrap().into(), expires_at: session["expires_at"].as_i64().unwrap() })
}
